- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.DoesNotHaveAnyFlagsCount has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.EnumerateNodesWithAllFlags has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.EnumerateNodesWithAnyFlags has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.EnumerateNodesWithoutAllFlags has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.EnumerateNodesWithoutAnyFlags has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;int&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;int&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithoutAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithoutAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;int&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithoutAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetIndicesWithoutAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;int&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;HierarchyNode&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;HierarchyNode&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithoutAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithoutAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;HierarchyNode&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithoutAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.GetNodesWithoutAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags, Span&lt;HierarchyNode&gt;\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAllFlags\(HierarchyNode, HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAllFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAllFlagsCount has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAnyFlags\(HierarchyNode, HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAnyFlags\(HierarchyNodeFlags\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HasAnyFlagsCount has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.Hierarchy has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.SetFlags\(HierarchyNode, HierarchyNodeFlags, bool\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewModel.ToggleFlags\(HierarchyNode, HierarchyNodeFlags, bool\) has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyViewNodesEnumerable has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Deprecated HierarchyWindow.InitializingView has been removed.

- Scripting: Removed: Removed the deprecated HierarchyViewModel.HierarchyFlattned.

- SRP Core: Added: Added SetBufferData to Raster/Unsafe command buffers and allow SetComputeBufferData for all APIs.

- SRP Core: Added: Enable Upscaling Framework by default.

- SRP Core: Obsoleted: Deprecating UnityEngine.Rendering.Pool and standarizing UnityEngine.Pool across SRP packages.

- Test Framework: Added: Added TestRunProfiler custom spans API.

- Test Framework: Removed: Removed the TestRunProfiler API from UnityEditor.TestTools.TestRunner.Api, which was added in an earlier 6000.7 pre-release.

- Text: Added: Added read-only glyph metrics API to FontAsset.
    (UUM-150976)

- uGUI: Added: Added `LayoutRebuilder.IsLayoutRebuildPending` and `LayoutGroup.IsLayoutPending()` to check whether a UI element still has a pending layout rebuild.

- uGUI: Changed: Selectable.s_Selectables changed from a Selectable array to List&lt;Selectable&gt;.
    ([UUM-150797](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150797))

- uGUI: Deprecated: Marked the `PositionAsUV1`, `TMP_TextElement_Legacy`, `FaceInfo_Legacy`, `TMP_Glyph`, `TMP_Sprite`, `KerningPair` and `KerningTable` classes as deprecated \(with a warning\).<br>
    Marked the `GlyphValueRecord_Legacy` struct as deprecated \(with a warning\).<br>
    Marked the `EventHandle` enum as deprecated \(with a warning\).<br>
    Marked `TMP_SpriteAsset.spriteInfoList` as deprecated \(with a warning\).<br>
    Marked `Scrollbar.ClickRepeat(PointerEventData eventData)` deprecated \(with a warning\).

- uGUI: Deprecated: Selectable.s_SelectableCount is now deprecated. Use Selectable.allSelectableCount instead.
    ([UUM-150797](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150797))

- uGUI: Obsoleted: Marked the `TMP_FontUtilities` class as obsolete \(with an error\).<br>
    Marked the `KerningPairKey` struct as obsolete \(with an error\).<br>
    Marked the `EventSystem.SetUITookitEventSystemOverride` method as obsolete \(with an error\).<br>
    Marked the `Graphic.m_CachedMesh`, `Graphic.m_CachedUvs` and `Graphic.useLegacyMeshGeneration` members as obsolete \(with an error\).

- uGUI: Removed: Removed `BaseMeshEffect.ModifyMesh(Mesh mesh)`.<br>
    Removed `BaseRaycaster.priority` property.<br>
    Removed `EventSystem.lastSelectedGameObject` property.<br>
    Removed `Graphic.OnPopulateMesh(Mesh m)`.<br>
    Removed `Image.eventAlphaThreshold` property.<br>
    Removed `InputField.onValueChange` property.<br>
    Removed `InputField.ScreenToLocal(Vector2 screen)`.<br>
    Removed `IGraphicEnabledDisabled` interface.<br>
    Removed `IMeshModifier.ModifyMesh(Mesh mesh)`.<br>
    Removed `Mask.OnSiblingGraphicEnabledDisabled()`.<br>
    Removed `PointerEventData.worldPosition` and `PointerEventData.worldNormal` properties.<br>
    Removed `RaycastResult.document` property.<br>
    Removed `Selectable.allSelectables` property.<br>
    Removed `ShaderUtilities.isInitialized` and `ShaderUtilities.GetShaderPropertyIDs()`.<br>
    Removed `StandaloneInputModule.InputMode` enum.<br>
    Removed `StandaloneInputModule.inputMode`, `StandaloneInputModule.allowActivationOnMobileDevice` and `StandaloneInputModule.forceModuleActive` properties.<br>
    Removed `StandaloneInputModule.ForceAutoSelect()`.<br>
    Removed `TextMeshPro.textContainer` property.<br>
    Removed `TMP_FontAsset.fontInfo` property.<br>
    Removed `TMP_Text.enableWordWrapping` and `TMP_Text.enableKerning` properties.<br>
    Removed `TMP_Text.SetText(string sourceText, bool syncTextInputBox = true)`.<br>
    Removed `TouchInputModule` class.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added api for measuring Min-content text.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added APIs FindPanelRenderer and FindUIDocument.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added ScrollView.touchScrollDirection to make the content move opposite to the pointer when dragged with touch or a tracked device.
    ([UUM-139596](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139596))

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added support to capture a `VisualElement` to a `RenderTexture`.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added the BoxShadow and StyleBoxShadow types, and the IStyle.boxShadow and IResolvedStyle.boxShadow properties.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added the PlayerLoop.PreUpdate.UIElementsPreparePanelRenderers player loop system, which attaches PanelRenderer content to its panel before Update.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added the public `UnityEngine.EventCommandNames` class, which provides the command name constants that the Editor sends with its command events, such as Copy, Paste, and FrameSelected.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added z-index USS property support for controlling the stacking order of overlapping elements within a stacking context. Enable via Project Settings &gt; UI Toolkit &gt; Experimental Renderer Features &gt; Enable Z-Index.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `IFormattingExtensionsToggle` to Smart Strings, which lets a source skip formatter extensions for the placeholder it resolved.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `ParserSettings.SelectorCharFilter` and `SelectorFilterType` to Smart Strings. Set the filter to `VisualUnicodeChars` to use characters from any script in a selector.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `PluralRules.RestoreDefault` to Smart Strings, which restores the built-in pluralization rules.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `UxmlSerializedDataPropertyDrawer`, `UxmlAttributeField`, and `UxmlAttributeFieldDecorator` to support custom property drawers for UI Toolkit elements in the Inspector.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Experimental VisualElement Components.

- URP: Added: Added 4 overloads for `DrawProceduralIndirect` that add a count parameter and an optional indirect count buffer for Multi Draw Indirect.

- URP: Added: Enable Upscaling Framework by default.

- UTR: Changed: Added CLI args to Integration and Runtime test runners to select target browser and platform.

- Video: Removed: Removed the deprecated `MovieTexture` API.

- Web: Added: Added new "Maximum Size Reduction" and "Maximum Size Reduction with LTO" Code Optimization settings. When enabled this will further optimize compiled IL2CPP code as well as apply extra link time optimizations \(these are distinct from LTO\). Testing across multiple projects and devices showed that compared to the default "Shorter Build Time" setting, "Maximum Size Reduction" reduces code size by an average of 28.50% at the cost of 5.25% frame time performance regression.

- Web: Added: Added `performance.measureUserAgentSpecificMemory` to web diagnostics overlay to calculate used JS memory.

- Windows: Added: Added GameWindow API support for Windows.



#### Changes

- 2D: Added Burst and Collections as dependency for com.unity.2d.tilemap package.

- 2D: Changed Tilemap component data structure for better compatibility with jobs.

- 2D: Refactored URP 2D shadow shaders into a consolidated multi-pass form with named passes \(`Self`, `UnshadowMark`, `UnshadowUnmark`, `ProjectedSelf`, `ProjectedUnshadow`\). Groundwork for upcoming custom-shader and ShaderGraph master node support; no user-visible rendering change.

- 2D: Renamed the Sprite "Physics Shape" API and Sprite Editor tab to "Physics Outline" to avoid confusion with PhysicsCore2D's `PhysicsShape`. The old API is deprecated but still works and auto-upgrades.

- 2D: Updated the Universal 2D project template to include 2D Physics Core settings.

- 2D: URP's three internal 2D shadow shaders are merged into a single five-pass shader; no user-visible rendering change.

- Android: Redesigned the Insets section in Player Settings to show a table with per-inset controls for Visible, Overlapping, and Appearance.

- Android: Updated the Android Gradle Plugin \(AGP\) version from 9.0.0 to 9.1.1.

- Android: Updated the Gradle version shipped with the Editor from 9.1.0 to 9.3.1.

- Asset Pipeline: Introducing a number of command-line arguments and environment variables related to the cache server \(making it possible to disable upload and download from/to the cache server of specific types of importers\).

- Documentation: Updated Color preferences doc page.

- Documentation: Updated the UI system comparison table to indicate that UITK now supports animation and in-scene authoring.

- Editor: Changed the Project Auditor Suppressed Issues preference to also accept spaces as a delimiter between issue IDs, in addition to commas and semicolons.

- Editor: Disabled static batching by default for new projects.

- Editor: Fast Enter Play Mode is now the default for projects created with the "-createProject" editor command line argument, from script code and for projects without a `ProjectSettings/EditorSettings.asset` file.  Affected projects will now default to the "Reload Scene Only" option when entering and exiting playmode in the editor rather than "Reload Domain and Scene". Existing projects are unaffected.<br>
    For more information see the online documentation:<br>
    - \[Configuring how Unity enters Play mode\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/6000.7/Documentation/Manual/configurable-enter-play-mode.html\)<br>
    - \[Enter Play mode without domain reload\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/6000.7/Documentation/Manual/domain-reloading.html\)<br>
    - \[Domain and scene reload execution order reference\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/6000.7/Documentation/Manual/configurable-enter-play-mode-details.html\).

- Editor: GTK : Added Support for variable condition transition drag n drop.

- Editor: GTK : Fixed an issue where the + button was not focusable.

- Editor: GTK: Fixed an issue where the transition counter was off center.

- Editor: Project Auditor: Added the ability to skip built-in analyzers for ones that have been migrated to the Project Auditor Rules package.

- Editor: Project Auditor: bumped Rules package minimum version to 2.0.0.

- Editor: Project Auditor: moved Domain Reload issues to Code pages.

- Editor: Project Auditor: new severity icons/colors.

- Editor: Project Auditor: removing opt-in checkbox for Roslyn.

- Editor: Removed the NetCoreRuntime from the Editor install.

- Editor: The macOS character palette shortcut now only opens the palette while a text field is being edited.

- Editor: Tweaked some Surface Cache quality presets.

- Editor: Updated the main toolbar UX, new design for toggles and better visualization of the groups of elements.

- Engine Diagnostics: Diagnostic Data Collection is now enabled by default for every build profile, including in upgraded projects, unless a build profile explicitly disables it.

- Engine Diagnostics: Diagnostic data is no longer collected in the Editor when the active build profile targets a platform that doesn't support Diagnostics.

- Engine Diagnostics: Editor crash and exception reporting to Diagnostics now follows the Diagnostic Data Collection setting of the active build profile.

- Engine Diagnostics: Removed the Diagnostics page from Project Settings &gt; Services. Diagnostic Data Collection is now configured for each build profile in the Build Profiles window, and Project Settings &gt; Services describes the diagnostic data that Unity collects.

- Engine Diagnostics: The Diagnostic Data Collection setting in the Build Profiles window now offers Enabled and Disabled, and disabling it no longer asks for confirmation.

- Entities: Added an option to hide Unity-Namespaced Systems in the System window.

- Entities: Added HasHierarchy tag component to TransformRef when it becomes part of a hierarchy.

- Entities: Bumped the scene serialized `CurrentFileFormatVersion` version number because the alignment of Entity is changed to 8 bytes.

- Entities: Display meta chunk entities matching queries in System window and Search window.

- Entities: Entities: com.unity.charactercontroller is now embedded as a core package in Unity.

- Entities: Fixed the incorrect icon for shared and chunk components in the System window.

- Entities: HierarchyColor from the Subscene monobehavior is now an error-level obsolete member.

- Entities: Moved the ImHex .hexpat generation used for binary debugging of .entities files, from the `UNITY_DOTS_IMHEX` script define to an editor preference \(Entities &gt; Debugging\).

- GI: Bake Reflection Probes no longer bakes Custom reflection probes, because it replaced their cubemap with a generated one. To bake a Custom probe, use the Bake button in its Inspector.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Lightmapping.BakeReflectionProbe now returns false and logs an error for Realtime reflection probes instead of writing a cubemap.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Migrate Surface Cache GI to use Ray Query instead of Ray Tracing Shaders.

- GI: Realtime Reflection Probes no longer use a baked fallback cubemap when Realtime Reflection Probes is disabled in Quality settings, including cubemaps baked before a probe was switched to Realtime.

- GI: Require "Raw" target for mesh vertex and index buffers. To achieve this, the "Default Mesh Buffer Target" setting should be set to "Raw" in Project Settings &gt; Graphics.

- GI: Surface Cache global illumination now uses only the LOD level selected by the LOD Group's Global Illumination LOD, instead of every level.

- Graph Toolkit: Passing `null` to the `dataType` parameter in the method `DataTypeStyleMapper.Register` now means to not show an icon at all for a type instead of showing a default icon.

- Graphics: Changed AsyncGPUReadback to reject a readback whose destination NativeArray is still in use by a running job, and report the error through the callback instead of proceeding.

- Graphics: Deprecated the Baked Occlusion Culling authoring workflow in the Editor. New projects no longer expose its authoring UI, while existing projects retain the workflow and baked data and display a deprecation notice. Consider migrating to GPU Occlusion Culling where supported.

- Graphics: Moved mesh deformation compute shaders to from default built-in resources to built-in extra resources.

- IL2CPP: Removed un-unused UnityAot2 Hashes.

- Installer: Added a -CPULightmapper=no command line switch to the Windows Editor installer that skips installing components only the CPU Progressive Lightmapper requires, currently the end-of-life Microsoft Visual C++ 2010 runtime.
    (UUM-149122)

- Mono: Removed assertions from ToFileDescriptor.
    ([UUM-145761](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145761))

- Multiplayer: Changed the run mode of a local player \(Scenario Control / Manual Control\) to be stored per developer instead of in the scenario asset, so it no longer travels with the asset in version control. Existing scenarios reset to Scenario Control the first time you open them in this version.

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: The `RoslynAnalyzerFix` asset post-processor \(and its assembly\) have been removed from the package. This deprecated hook did not serve a purpose anymore since the analyzer was removed from the package in version 2.5.0.
    (UUM-146183)

- Netcode for Entities: Always automatically generate the `NetcodeConfig` singleton. This is a breaking change for any project that uses `EntityManager.CreateSingleton<NetcodeConfig>()` to create the singleton explicitly. You can update your code to use `SystemAPI.GetSingleton<NetcodeConfig>()` instead.

- Netcode for Entities: N4E is now a regular editor-independent package, where previously it was built-in.

- Package: Added localization package 2.0. This version is designed to work with the built in localization module.

- Package Manager: Changed the signature window to be more consistent and display more information about the installed package.

- Package Manager: Help boxes learn more links are at the end of the text in the Package Manager window.

- Package Manager: Marked the Analytics Library package \(com.unity.analytics\) as deprecated. Migrate to Unity Analytics \(com.unity.services.analytics\).

- Package Manager: Updated the icons for Installed, Refresh, Installed as dependency and removed the icons for available for import, and available for download.

- Performance Test Framework: PerformanceTestRunInfo.json and PerformanceTestRunSettings.json are no longer generated during player builds that do not include test assemblies, as they are only read by performance tests running inside the player. Stale files left behind by an aborted test build are removed before such builds instead of being included in them.

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsComposer.AddLayer", "PhysicsComposer.ToPolygons", "CircleGeometry.ToPolygons" and "CapsuleGeometry.ToPolygons" now log the standard validation warning instead of throwing "ArgumentOutOfRangeException" when given no geometry, fewer than three vertices or an invalid "curveStride".
    ([UUM-156367](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156367))

- Physics 2D: Changed the default value of `PhysicsBodyDefinition.transformWriteMode` from `Off` to `Interpolation`. Existing `PhysicsCoreSettings2D` assets are unaffected; only newly created assets use the new default.

- Physics 2D: Changed `PhysicsWorld.Explode` so that it no longer applies an impulse to trigger shapes by default.

- Prefabs: Loading a prefab or scene that stores prefab data in the format written by Unity 3.3 and earlier now logs a deprecation warning naming the asset. Save the asset to store it in the current format.

- Scripting: Added an infobox to Build Profiles window that informs you of the new Managed Code Variants feature. This infobox can be permanently dismissed by pressing the "Dismiss" button.

- Scripting: Added CoreCLR runtime, .NET base class library, and managed Unity engine PDBs to the public symbol server.

- Serialization: Changed the Dictionary Inspector warning about ignored entries to offer both Select First and Select All buttons when more than one entry is ignored.
    (UUM-151199)

- Serialization: Dictionary fields can now use a class or struct key, or a struct value, that contains a SerializeReference field.

- Serialization: Renamed the Reset to Defaults item in the Dictionary Inspector header context menu to Reset Layout, to make it clear that it restores only the drawer layout and not the dictionary contents.
    (UUM-151199)

- Shadergraph: Moved the Particle Shader Graph sample from the HDRP package to the Shader Graph package.

- Shadergraph: Removed the Back To Front and Front To Back Render Face options from URP Shader Graphs. Shader Graphs and materials saved with these options now render front faces only.

- Shaders: Surface shaders are now marked as deprecated.

- UI Toolkit: Always show the UXML document link and asset actions in the element inspector header outside the UI Stage.

- UI Toolkit: AttributeOverrides are now shown as disabled during main stage authoring.

- UI Toolkit: Changed Constant Physical Size panels to account for the Game view's Low Resolution Aspect Ratios setting when resolving scale.

- UI Toolkit: Changed the Text attribute field to 3 lines by default instead of 1 when editing in the UI Builder or Scene inspector.

- UI Toolkit: Changed theme style sheets to rank above Unity's built-in styles regardless of selector specificity; the previous behavior can be restored with the Legacy Theme Priority setting in Project Settings &gt; UI Toolkit.

- UI Toolkit: Changed touch dragging of ScrollView content on world-space panels so the content follows the finger pixel for pixel, regardless of the Pixels Per Unit setting.
    ([UUM-139596](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139596))

- UI Toolkit: Changed z-index transitions between auto and an integer to switch at the midpoint of the transition instead of interpolating from 0, to match CSS.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed -unity-slice properties not recording or playing back in UI Animation Clips.

- UI Toolkit: The Editor theme style sheet is now applied to the root of the Editor window panel instead of EditorWindow.rootVisualElement, so it no longer appears in rootVisualElement.styleSheets.

- URP: Allow skipping of shadow, prepass, opaque, and transparent passes when the culling/layer masks would mean nothing would be drawn.

- URP: Mata passes for GI no longer requires _EMISSION but checks purely from gi material flags.

- URP: Renamed the Light Falloff Mode option from Linear to Quadratic.

- URP: Reordered the Screen Space Ambient Occlusion pass when After Opaque is enabled. In previous versions, it used to run in the `BeforeRenderingTransparents` point in the frame. Now it runs in the `AfterRenderingOpaques` point in the frame.

- VFX Graph: Moved the "Restart VFX" shortcut from Shift+Space to Slash.
    ([UUM-150458](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150458))

- VFX Graph: Provided clear messaging that custom light probes are not fully supported in VFX Graph yet.
    ([UUM-150046](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150046))

- Web: Further reduce the code size of Web builds using the "Disk Size" Code Optimization settings.<br>
    Testing across multiple projects and devices showed that compared to the default "Shorter Build Time" setting, "Disk Size" reduced code size by an average of 8.75%, with this change the reduction is now an average of 21.35%. As always these settings are a trade-off between size and runtime performance, the same testing showed that the average frame time performance regression grows from 2.13% to 4.56% with the performance regression being reduced when LTO is enabled.

- Web: Move progressive asset loading data files to Build directory of player build output.

- Web: Updated deprecation message for WebAssembly 2023.

- Windows: DirectStorage is now enabled by default in new projects for the Windows Standalone player.

- Windows: Windows builds now link the Visual C++ runtime dynamically instead of statically, and Unity copies the required Visual C++ runtime DLLs \(such as `vcruntime140.dll` and `msvcp140.dll`\) into the build output next to the executable. A new "Copy Visual C++ runtime libraries" option under Publishing Settings \(on by default\) lets you skip copying them if you install the redistributable another way, for example via your own installer or a distribution platform such as Steam.

- XR: Updated XR Core Utilities package to version 6.0.0 in order to match Unity version.

- XR: Updated XR Legacy Input Helpers package to version 6.0.0 in order to match Unity version.

- XR: XR Management package version updated to 6.0.0.



#### Fixes

- 2D: Fix case where ideo is not rendered when the VideoPlayer GameObject is disabled and re-enabled after playback starts while using Material Override render mode
    ([UUM-141113](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141113))

- 2D: Fix Crash on "pqInit" when SpriteShapeUtility.Generate is given a closed shape with two coincident control points.<br>
    Note this API is specific to an old Spriteshape package and will be deprecated in an upcoming version. Please refer to SpriteShapeGenerator.cs in the Spriteshape package for any future use of Triangulation.
    ([UUM-156213](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156213))

- 2D: Fix Crash on SpriteAtlas::GetSpritesScripting when passing null to SpriteAtlas.GetSprites
    ([UUM-155330](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155330))

- 2D: Fix editor crash in SpriteDataAccessExtensions::SetVertexCount when the count is negative
    ([UUM-154126](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154126))

- 2D: Fix issues where Unsaved Changes Detected pop-up are doubled after editing Sprite Atlas asset if it was open on multiple Inspector windows and choosing the Discard option
    ([UUM-151573](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151573))

- 2D: Fixed a "No script asset for ShadowCasterPath" warning when you undo or redo a Shadow Caster 2D shape edit in Play mode.
    ([UUM-152935](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152935))

- 2D: Fixed a case where in In 3D Editor mode, textures did not generate Sprite subassets when imported as SpriteSprite Generation.
    ([UUM-142920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142920))

- 2D: Fixed a crash when entering Play Mode with a Tilemap whose tiles re-enter the Tilemap API during StartUp.
    ([UUM-153542](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153542))

- 2D: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown every frame by a 2D Light of type Provider whose Light2DProvider returns no mesh.

- 2D: Fixed a NullReferenceException when a Light 2D created from a script is set to Freeform without a shape path.
    ([UUM-154810](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154810))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a LineRenderer or TrailRenderer with Mask Interaction set to None was clipped by a Sprite Mask when another LineRenderer or TrailRenderer using the same material had Mask Interaction enabled.
    ([UUM-158602](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158602))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a Point Light 2D lost its cookie sprite when a project saved in Unity 6.0 or 6.3 was opened in a newer version.
    ([UUM-156931](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156931))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where changing the SpriteAtlas option in EditorSettings results in render artifacts.
    (UUM-139228)

- 2D: Fixed an issue where creating a Tile from the Tile Palette failed when the destination folder name contained a dot.
    ([UUM-158114](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158114))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where rotating a multi-layer brush on a Hexagon Tilemap merged its Z layers and lost tiles.
    ([UUM-156684](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156684))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where undoing an erased Tile did not restore the Tile's GameObject.

- 2D: Fixed an issue where `Tilemap.tilemapPositionsChanged` and `tilemapTileChanged` callbacks didn't invoke when `SetTiles` changed only the transform or color of a tile.

- 2D: Fixed an issue where `TilemapRenderer` inherited normal maps from other Tiles when a Tile did not have a normal map.

- 2D: Fixed ArgumentException when using overlay camera in 2D Renderer.

- 2D: Fixed case of Tile Palette Brush Picks overlay turning itself off in the Scene View when the user clears the search field in the overlay \(for example by pressing Backspace\).

- 2D: Fixed case where crash with multiple stack traces when Sprites with a high resolution get Sliced to a Grid with a small cell size in the Sprite Editor.

- Accessibility: Fixed accessibility node queries logging a main-thread error to the console on Android when a screen reader is enabled.

- Accessibility: Fixed double state announcement for `AccessibilityRole.Toggle` nodes on Android.
    ([UUM-146950](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146950))

- Accessibility: Fixed the Accessibility Hierarchy Viewer displaying the previous Play session's accessibility hierarchy after exiting Play mode.

- Accessibility: Fixed the main application window also being provided for secondary windows for UI Automation clients.

- Accessibility: Fixed the screen reader being slow to navigate away from `AccessibilityRole.Dropdown` nodes on Android.
    ([UUM-146949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146949))

- Accessibility: Fixed the Windows player crashing at shutdown once a UI Automation client \(e.g. a screen reader\) has interacted with the application.
    (UUM-146676)

- Accessibility: Fixed the Windows player crashing on quit when a UI Automation client \(e.g. a screen reader\) is running.
    ([UUM-157903](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157903))

- Accessibility: Fixed `AssistiveSupport.screenReaderStatusChanged` not being supported by Narrator \(the Windows built-in screen reader\).
    ([UUM-147175](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147175))

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed Adaptive Performance samples in XR so the user's camera faces the sample content.

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed indexer logic to handle when low utilization and thermal values cause performanceAction to become StateAction.Increase, indicating we have headroom to increase quality.

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed Visual Scripting nodes \(bottleneck, thermal, performance level, boost, performance mode, and scaler events\) no longer firing in the player after Adaptive Performance moved into the engine module. The events are now forwarded to the Visual Scripting `EventBus` from the adaptive performance samples package assembly, where the `VISUAL_SCRIPTING_ENABLED` define is active.
    (UUM-135587)

- Adaptive Performance: Gate Adaptive Performance settings during package operations.
    ([UUM-140197](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140197))

- AI: As a result of an unsupported configuration, a NavMeshAgent would try to separate from its own NavMeshObstacle component by jumping in arbitrary directions. Now the agent moves in one stable direction.
    (UUM-141803)

- AI: Fixed editor crash when baking a nav mesh that includes objects that result in an extremely large bounds.
    ([UUM-134462](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134462))

- AI: Fixed the order of the two portal points returned for a NavMesh link, so that the first one is on the left and the second on the right also for a path that leaves the link. This applies to the results of `NavWorld.GetPortalPoints` and `NavMeshQuery.GetPortalPoints`.
    ([UUM-152707](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152707))

- AI: NavMeshAgents created or teleported inside box NavMeshObstacles could not move out of the obstacles.
    ([UUM-146477](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146477))

- AI: The `sourceBounds` from a NavMeshData object generated far from origin were incorrectly including the origin. These bounds now wrap tightly around the meshes used for building the NavMesh.
    ([UUM-152672](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152672))

- AI: Updated nav agent to ignore and warning about a path being set from a navigation surface not matching the agent's Id.
    ([UUM-108989](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-108989))

    ([UUM-150216](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150216))

- Android: Fixed a crash in LoadFontFamilyNamesFromFontFile on Android 10 devices when querying OS font fallbacks.
    (UUM-147085)

- Android: Fixed a crash in LoadFontFamilyNamesFromFontFile on Android 10 devices when querying OS font fallbacks.
    ([UUM-147144](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147144))

- Android: Fixed a crash when an app is relaunched with `FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK` while it is already running.
    ([UUM-158675](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158675))

- Android: Fixed a crash when calling `AnimatorController.CreateNewStateMachineBehaviour` with a null MonoScript. It now throws an `ArgumentNullException` instead.
    ([UUM-150813](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150813))

- Android: Fixed a false-positive Java heap leak failure in the Android player-unloading integration test by excluding first-reload warmup from the baseline.
    (UUM-150246)

- Android: Fixed a warning incorrectly appearing in Preferences &gt; External Tools when using a custom Android SDK Tools path.

- Android: Fixed alignment issues in Android External Tools settings panel.
    (UUM-148378)

- Android: Fixed an approximately 7 MB native memory leak per Application.Unload cycle when using GameActivity.

- Android: Fixed an issue on OpenGL where Unity's render thread would attach/detach to the JVM multiple times in a frame, causing unnecessary allocations.
    (UUM-155309)

- Android: Fixed an issue that caused lag in the Unity preferences text fields when Android support is installed.
    (UUM-147435)

- Android: Fixed an issue where pressing the Back key twice was required to fully close the touch screen keyboard and its input field on Android 17 when Predictive Back support was disabled.
    (UUM-153547)

- Android: Fixed Android native libraries having a GNU_RELRO segment that was not aligned to 16 KB. `libc++_shared.so` will stay unaligned until the next NDK update.
    ([UUM-156603](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156603))

- Android: Fixed Android version labels to match Google's naming.
    (UUM-150412)

- Android: Fixed CanBuildWithSymlinkSources flakiness caused by parameterized runs sharing a temp export directory.
    (UUM-149303)

- Android: Fixed case of system bars appearance resetting to Light automatically on Android 11 when System Bars Behavior is set to Dark.

- Android: Fixed case of system bars not hiding automatically on Android 11 when System Bars Behavior is set to "Show Transient Bars By Swipe".
    ([UUM-149900](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149900))

- Android: Fixed freeze when relaunching GameActivity immediately after unload in UaaL mode.
    (UUM-149857)

- Android: Fixed mouse movement producing scroll values when the cursor is locked.
    ([UUM-156720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156720))

- Android: Fixed problem with WebCamTexture not restoring on Android when "Run Without Focus" is set.
    (UUM-149681)

- Android: Fixed SIGSEGV on Application.Quit\(\) when the cursor was ever set.
    ([UUM-153932](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153932))

- Android: Fixed split APKs by target architecture all receiving the same version code.
    ([UUM-144372](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/arm64-and-armv7-apks-return-the-same-version-code-when-split-apks-by-target-architecture-is-enabled))

    ([UUM-155415](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155415))

- Android: Fixed the Compile SDK Version dropdown in Android Player Settings listing API levels that are lower than the minimum supported compile API level.

- Android: Fixed WebCamTexture taking several seconds to resume after the app regains focus or another app releases the camera, for example in split-screen mode.
    (UUM-155066)

- Android: Improved the "Missing Required API Level" dialog.
    (UUM-150206)

- Android: Negative values for ```Screen.brightness``` now restore the device's preferred screen brightness.
    ([UUM-145925](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145925))

- Android: Re-enabled Java heap leak check in AndroidPlayerUnloading integration tests.

    (UUM-143152)

- Android: Relaxed AGP 9's stricter R8 keep rule enforcement.
    ([UUM-147136](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147136))

- Android: Stop requiring targetSdk's platform SDK to be installed in CheckAndroidSDK.cs.

- Android: Updated the bundled Android SDK cmdline-tools to 21.0, so emulators for Android 17 \(API 37.0\) created with its avdmanager start correctly.
    (UUM-157110)

- Android: `SystemInfo.processorModel` and `SystemInfo.processorManufacturer` now return unknown on Android API levels below 32 instead of a warning string, matching the behaviour on other platforms.
    (UUM-150389)

- Animation: Added ability to modify multiple tangents at once in a keyframe selection of the Curve Editor.

- Animation: Adjusted test to prevent unstable behaviour.
    (UUM-147321)

- Animation: Animator.ResetControllerState\(true\) didn't reset non-float parameters.
    ([UUM-146636](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146636))

- Animation: Crash when calling AnimationUtility.SetAdditiveReferencePose\` with a null clip or referenceClip.
