    ([UUM-150938](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150938))

- Animation: Fixed a crash when calling `AnimationPlayableOutput.SetSortingOrder` on an invalid/default `AnimationPlayableOutput`. It now throws a `NullReferenceException` instead.
    ([UUM-150941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150941))

- Animation: Fixed a crash where providing a default/invalid `AnimationPlayableOutput` to `AnimationPlayableOutputExtensions.SetAnimationStreamSource`, would cause a crash.
    ([UUM-150939](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150939))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where AnimationEvent-related tooltips in the Animation Window were not rendered correctly while in Bitmap text rendering mode.
    ([UUM-141839](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/animation-event-tooltips-are-not-rendered-properly-when-using-the-bitmap-text-rendering-mode-in-the-animation-window))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where Animator transition copy paste would not paste interruption parameters.
    ([UUM-143599](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/interruption-source-ordered-interruption-and-can-transition-to-self-fields-are-not-pasted-to-transition-settings-when-pasting-transition-parameters))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where Animator window's Layers dropdown \(in compact mode\) would be extremely wide when layers have very long names.
    ([UUM-131557](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131557))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where clicking in the "Edit Key" fields of the Animation Window curve view wouldn't deselect the text in the field.
    ([UUM-140166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140166))

- Animation: Fixed flickering of area filter in the search bar.
    ([UUM-144937](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144937))

- Animation: Fixed function name changing on Animation Event inspector on multi selection when editing another field.
    ([UUM-144456](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/animation-events-switch-to-the-same-function-when-toggling-require-receiver-while-the-animation-events-are-multi-selected))

- Animation: Fixed GUIStyle warnings when clicking on \\"Add Properties\\" in Animation Window.
    ([UUM-148497](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148497))

- Animation: Fixed hover feedback on curve label and curve dropdown in the Animation Window.
    ([UUM-136071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136071))

- Animation: Fixed MecanimDataWasBuilt assertion when building blend tree with a legacy AnimationClip.
    ([UUM-142427](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assertion-failed-on-expression-mecanimdatawasbuilt-errors-thrown-in-the-console-window-when-changing-animation-type-to-legacy-in-import-settings))

- Animation: Fixed methods with an AnimationEventInfo parameter not being listed in the Function dropdown of the Animation Event Inspector.
    ([UUM-155319](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155319))

- Animation: Fixed Null Reference Exception when selection Object drop destination from multiple choices in the dopesheet editor.
    ([UUM-148386](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148386))

- Animation: Fixed play button enabled state not updating when another Animation Window changes preview.
    ([UUM-145360](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/play-button-is-disabled-in-a-second-animation-window-after-pausing-and-disabling-preview-in-the-first-animation-window))

- Animation: Fixed possible hash collision in AvatarAutoMapper.
    (UUM-146209)

- Animation: Fixed search field overflowing vertically when EnableQueryBuilder is disabled.
    ([UUM-148577](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148577))

- Animation: Fixed the Select Game Object window not appearing when selecting Other... from the Animation Preview Model dropdown a second time.
    ([UUM-148566](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148566))

- Animation: Reworked Animation Playable Output bind and unbind validation to prevent potential crashes.
    ([UUM-146750](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146750))

- Animation: Updated the colors of the Animation window slider for light mode.
    (UUM-148485)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed a player crash when instantiating a prefab from an AssetBundle that was reached through a reference to a child object of a prefab in another AssetBundle.
    (UUM-141605)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed a player hang or crash with heap corruption on application quit when Engine Diagnostics \("Build Diagnostics Data"\) is enabled and an AssetBundle has been loaded.

- Asset Bundles: Fixed AssetBundle build reports not recording time or log messages for pre/post-process build callbacks.
    (UUM-143933)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed AssetBundle content hashes changing between builds of unchanged content with Addressables or the Scriptable Build Pipeline, causing players to redownload unmodified bundles. Affected bundles get a new hash on the first build after upgrading.
    ([UUM-148841](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148841))

- Asset Import: Fixed an Editor crash in Transform when an asset reimport unloaded objects that were still loaded.
    (UUM-126651)

- Asset Import: Fixed default presets for importers provided by a built-in module, such as SVGImporter, no longer being applied after restarting the project.
    ([UUM-146265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146265))

- Asset Import: Fixed issue with cache mechanism of compressed textures of the Accelerator not tracking texture dependency on BC7 compressor when fetching from cache. Textures were not being reimported. Improved hash calculation.
    (UUM-146105)

- Asset Import: Fixed the Linux Editor sometimes exiting while importing crunch-compressed textures, with "crn_threading_pthreads.cpp\(194\): Failure: semaphore: sem_wait\(\) or sem_timedwait\(\) failed" in the log. The crunch compressor treated an interrupted wait as a fatal error; it now retries the wait.
    ([UUM-150055](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150055))

- Asset Pipeline: Expect Worker\# in native importers when importer runs OOP.

- Asset Pipeline: Fix to set StandbyImportWorkerCount and DesiredImportWorkerCount to minimum 1. Affects command line argument, setting via script, setting via ProjectSettings.
    ([UUM-150028](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150028))

- Asset Pipeline: Fix to skip MaterialValidation test with oop importing, as the Material isn't unloaded in the main process in that case.

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed "ScriptableSingleton ... already exists" warnings appearing in the Console when a package creates its settings asset, for example after installing the XR Composition Layers package.
    ([UUM-147680](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147680))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash on Editor shutdown caused by an asset import worker timer callback running after the worker manager was destroyed.
    (UUM-134447)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when an AssetModificationProcessor called AssetDatabase.Refresh from OnWillSaveAssets while the Editor wrote an asset's .meta file, for example during AssetDatabase.ForceReserializeAssets.
    ([UUM-156706](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156706))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when calling `AssetDatabase.ForceReserializeAssets()` more than once in a row. When reserializing a dirty asset, `ReloadFromDisk` awoke the reloaded object while the serialization read namespace was still active, so a reference to a not-yet-loaded dependency \(such as a built-in shader\) hit the recursive-serialization guard and left the object in a corrupt state; the next `ForceReserializeAssets` call then crashed while reloading it.
    ([UUM-138620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138620))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when opening a project that contained a manager asset holding an object of the wrong type.
    ([UUM-153271](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153271))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when opening a project with a corrupted Library.
    ([UUM-153198](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153198))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a random Editor crash on shutdown caused by a data race in the asset database script artifact bookkeeping.
    (UUM-142994)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a regression that slowed memory snapshot capture on Windows.
    (UUM-150454)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an allocation crash that could occur under heavy import loads.
    ([UUM-146671](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146671))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an Editor crash in `WriteObjectToVector` / `CreateOrReloadInspectorCopy` that could occur when entering Play Mode.
    ([UUM-112617](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-crashes-on-unity-writeobjecttovector-when-entering-into-the-play-mode))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an import issue when creating a plugin folder \(.androidlib, .bundle, .plugin, .framework,<br>
    .xcframework\) and adding new files in it.
    (UUM-139172)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where a "File exists" error was shown when creating a project or duplicating a folder on an exFAT drive on macOS.
    ([UUM-150634](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150634))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetImportContext.GetArtifactFilePath returned a path that could not be read with System.IO when the artifact was stored by the data store.
    ([UUM-150044](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150044))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where content could fail to commit to the data store after the Editor was force-quit during an import.
    (UUM-151756)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where downloading from Accelerator could fail to close open file handles when finished.
    (UUM-151405)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where macOS editors could hang when the Asset Database rescanned an absolute path.
    (UUM-147982)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where memory misalignment could cause in-memory atomics to fail.
    (UUM-147041)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where performance could degrade and eventually fail when the Accelerator was under heavy load.
    (UUM-146082)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where small artifact data types could be incorrectly garbage collected, leading to leaks and even corruption.
    (UUM-146807)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash during a domain reload or on quit while asset previews were loading.
    ([UUM-141036](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141036))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash when loading or force-reserializing a PlayerSettings asset saved by an old version of Unity.
    ([UUM-138620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138620))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the editor could hang if the Unity.DataStore process was killed.
    ([UUM-149968](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149968))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor crashed on launch when the project was on an exFAT drive on macOS.
    ([UUM-156738](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156738))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed asset previews staying white after a referenced texture is deleted and restored with the same GUID.
    ([UUM-138622](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138622))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed AssetDatabase.CreateFolder returning a GUID of all zeros instead of an empty string when the folder could not be created.
    ([UUM-154150](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154150))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed Editor crash when the Unity Version Control Diff window shows a modified prefab that contains nested prefab instances.
    ([UUM-155069](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155069))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed hang at startup when directory monitoring doesn't receive expected notification about changed file\(s\).
    ([UUM-149019](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149019))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed ShaderImporter indeterminism issues and bump importer versions.

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed the AssetDatabase re-importing assets endlessly when an asset depended on a default asset produced after an import crash.
    (UUM-146505)

- Asset Pipeline: Include ShaderImporter, RayTracingShaderImporter and ComputeShaderImporter to run out of process when ProjectSettings-&gt;Parallel Importing is enabled.

- Asset Pipeline: Optimised a number of hot paths for asset importing and content building.

- Asset Pipeline: Recompute serialized singleton overlays for versioned assets if no files changed on disk.

- Audio: Bug was already fixed. Added a test.
    ([UUM-147800](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147800))

- Audio: Fixed a bug on macOS where audio playback would slow down when the microphone was being used by the system.
    ([UUM-149880](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149880))

- Audio: Fixed a fatal error in the Editor when an audio output device was reconnected after Unity failed to resume audio output.
    ([UUM-158166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158166))

- Audio: Fixed a rare crash in `Sound.getOpenState` caused by a data race during asynchronous sound loading.
    ([UUM-145498](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-in-fmod-when-using-load-in-background))

- Audio: Fixed an assertion \(m_ModifiedClips.empty\(\)\) that was logged on exiting Play Mode after playing an AudioSource whose clip had loaded while Unity Audio was toggled off and back on during Play Mode.
    ([UUM-148749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148749))

- Audio: Fixed assertion about access version needing be odd when acquiring locks in an audio subsystem, happening after the editor was left running for many days.
    ([UUM-146734](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146734))

- Audio: Fixed AudioClips created with AudioClip.Create losing their sample data and logging "Cannot attach thread to NULL scripting domain" errors when entering Play mode with domain and scene reload disabled.
    ([UUM-156073](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156073))

- Audio: Fixed AudioSettings.driverCapabilities returning undefined value when using the Enhanced Audio Foundation.
    (UUM-148718)

- Audio: Fixed case of audiomixer fader clicking in playmode.
    ([UUM-150250](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150250))

- Audio: Fixed crash when pausing AudioRandomContainer containing streaming clips on some platforms.
    (UUM-148440)

- Audio: Fixed invalid LoadState in some audio clips after Audio Settings reset.
    ([UUM-147700](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147700))

- Audio: Fixed microphone recording continuing when the application loses focus with Run In Background disabled. Recording is now muted while the application is paused.
    (UUM-143384)

- Audio: Fixed `RootOutputInstance.IRealtime` not properly running through Burst if requested, even though Burst would compile and show the associated code in the Inspector.
    ([UUM-145622](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scriptable-audio-processor-rootoutput-isnt-bursted-at-runtime))

- Audio: Prevents FMOD validation warnings when typing values in the System Sample Rate field, waits until full value is typed before validating.
    ([UUM-136464](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136464))

- Bug Reporter: Fixed crash when pack location is read-only.

- Bug Reporter: Fixed the Player.log location.

- Bug Reporter: Improved the resilience pipeline when fetching the Editor version for a bug report.

- Build Pipeline: Added support to reserve capacity of PersistentManager.Remapper in Memory Settings \(Main Allocators &gt; Other Allocators &gt; Remapper Initial Capacity\).
    (UUM-136275)

- Build Pipeline: Building will now fail if scene callbacks throw an exception or crash, regardless of strict mode.
    (UUM-143730)

- Build Pipeline: Command line Player builds no longer spend extra time on a build analytics step that could not complete. The step ran before the build data it reads was written, so it failed on every Player build and collected nothing.
    (UUM-147655)

- Build Pipeline: Content Directory builds are properly stripping LODs and Editor-Only Objects from a scene before calling the scene build callbacks and batching the static meshes.
    (UUM-145061)

- Build Pipeline: ContentLayout.json \(produced by content directory builds\) now includes an OutputLFID field on each LoadableObjectIds entry. Previously only the source object's identifier was recorded, so tools could not correlate a loadable with its exact object in the build output without also reading BuildManifest.json. The ContentLayout.json schema version is now 2.
    (UUM-145565)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue in the Build Analysis window where all columns of a table could be hidden, which left an empty list that could still be scrolled and selected.
    ([UUM-143786](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143786))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where VideoClips referenced by Content Directory builds failed to load at runtime on macOS.
    (UUM-147430)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed Build And Run launching the player before the IPostprocessBuildWithReport and IPostprocessBuildWithContext callbacks had run.
    (UUM-154495)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed build step labels in the Build Analysis window showing internal method names instead of readable text.
    ([UUM-150888](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150888))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed builds added to the build history while the Build Analysis window was closed not appearing in the build list when the window was opened.

- Build Pipeline: Fixed sequential command line builds started with -activeBuildProfile compiling scripts for the previously active platform.
    (UUM-150775)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the Build Analysis inspector reopening on asset selection after being closed.
    ([UUM-154999](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154999))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the build count in the Build Analysis window footer not following the search filter. The footer now shows the number of matching builds while searching, and shows the build limit count only from 70% of the limit.
    ([UUM-150885](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150885))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the Build Details section of the Build Analysis window not showing a build's related builds.
    (UUM-155781)

- Build Pipeline: Improved the Build Callbacks documentation to mention the new AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene API.

- Build Pipeline: Moved "Enable MSBuild Compilation" project setting into editor's pane.

- Build Pipeline: Reenabled unit test SwitchCodeOptimization_WhenScriptsCompilationError_SwitchIsNotAllowed.
    (UUM-110351)

- Build System: Bee_backend's stale output removal should not recheck the same file indefinitely.
    (UUM-158568)

- Build System: Don't hang the Editor when a previous bee_backend is still running.
    ([UUM-142773](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-log-write-logfile-when-more-than-one-copy-of-bee-backend-is-running))

- Build System: Fixed an issue with DotNetFrameworkDependentApp on CSharpTools by not using it because it wasn't needed.

- Build System: Fixed case of the IL2CPP Analytics tool exiting with a JsonException after a successful build when the system clock moved backwards during the build.
    ([UUM-150019](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150019))

- Burst: Burst compilation was skipped for jobs discovered via \[JobProducerType\] \(such as IJobChunk\) in Player builds using the High Managed Stripping Level, causing them to silently fall back to managed code. \(\).
    ([UUM-147717](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147717))

- Burst: Ensure the bundle is resigned correctly when running under X64 process root.
    ([UUM-149014](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149014))

- Burst: Fixed a rare compilation crash caused by a data race in portable-PDB debug info handling during parallel compilation.

- Burst: Fixed an issue where enabling or disabling UI Toolkit Live Reload on the **Burst Inspector** window logged a window serialization error.
    ([UUM-148278](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148278))

- Burst: Fixed that some of Burst's Windows assemblies were not Authenticode-signed. All Burst-authored binaries are now signed.

- Burst: Fixed the Burst Inspector disassembly search bar requiring a second Ctrl/CMD + F press to reopen after being closed.
    ([UUM-144286](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144286))

- Burst: Structs with auto-layout can now be used in Burst as long as they are not a part of a method's signature or a job's struct. This allows tuples to be used in structs that don't cross the managed-native barrier again.

- Cache Server: Fixed command-line arguments for the cache server not being sent properly to the asset import worker.

- Cinemachine: Added a prompt asking the user whether to save their scene before upgrading a project to Cinemachine 3.
    ([UUM-148112](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148112))

- Cinemachine: Changed static reset in CinemachineCore to happen during SubsystemRegistration.
    ([UUM-148700](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148700))

- Cinemachine: Fixed C\# scripts referencing moved Cinemachine APIs not being automatically upgraded from the Cinemachine namespace to Unity.Cinemachine when upgrading a project.
    (UUM-147045)

- Cinemachine: Fixed disappearing foldout properties in Cinemachine Orbital Follow inspector.
    ([UUM-143630](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143630))

- Cinemachine: Fixed invalid obsolete InvalidPathCache call in CinemachineConfiner.
    ([UUM-147086](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147086))

- Cinemachine: Fixed SerializeReference serialization warnings when adding a modifier to CinemachineFreeLookModifier.
    ([UUM-147115](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147115))

- Cinemachine: OrbitalFollow cameras using the InheritPosition blend hint with position damping now inherit the outgoing camera's direction correctly, and jerk less on the blend back.
    ([UUM-149754](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149754))

- Code Editor: Fixed the "Reset argument" button in Preferences &gt; External Tools so it updates the "External Script Editor Args" field immediately instead of only after a focus change.
    (UUM-150210)

- Core: Fixed case of `DataStreamReader` and `DataStreamWriter` intermittently corrupting packed data on 32-bit ARM \(armeabi-v7a\) players under IL2CPP. The bit-packing fast path read and wrote its 64-bit word by casting the buffer pointer and dereferencing it \(`*(ulong*)(buffer + byteIndex)`\) at an arbitrary byte offset; that is an unaligned 64-bit access, which is undefined behavior on ARMv7 and silently returned mangled bytes. The word is now transferred with a byte-wise copy \(`UnsafeUtility.MemCpy`\), which is alignment-safe on every architecture, and folds back to a single load/store on targets that allow unaligned access.
    (UUM-138845)

- Core: Fixed crash when calling `GameObjectUtility.GetStaticEditorFlags`, `SetStaticEditorFlags`, `AreStaticEditorFlagsSet`, `GetNavMeshArea`, `SetNavMeshArea`, `GetNavMeshLayer`, or `SetNavMeshLayer` with a null `GameObject` argument. These now throw `ArgumentNullException` instead.
    ([UUM-151059](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151059))

- Core: Fixed Leak Detection reporting false leaks on the first Domain Reload after switching from EnabledWithStackTrace to Enabled.
    ([UUM-108870](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-108870))

- Core: Fixed `Transform` serialized values not being properly initialized when cloning GameObjects.
    ([UUM-131305](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/transform-corruption-and-slash-or-crash-on-physx-createcharactercontroller-when-spawning-physics-objects-into-prefab-stages))

- Core: Revert problematic string operation optimizations.
    (UUM-146910)

- CoreCLR: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- CoreCLR: Fixed Linux CoreCLR player builds including the CoreCLR runtime's native debug symbols \(.so.dbg files\), which made builds several hundred MB larger.

- DX12: Avoid creating D3D12 device twice.
    (UUM-147044)

- DX12: Enabled URP_Lighting test suite.

- DX12: Fixed a crash on DirectX 12 when a large texture, such as a big dynamic font atlas, was updated many times in a single frame.
    ([UUM-140070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140070))

- DX12: Fixed a DirectX 12 validation error caused by incorrect resource-state tracking on async command lists.
    (UUM-144862)

- DX12: Fixed a graphics validation error \(category 9, id 538\) at BeginRenderPass for render pass input attachments when using split-threaded native graphics jobs.
    (UUM-144553)

- DX12: Fixed a race on DX12 where a native rendering plugin could write to a texture or buffer before its initial data upload had completed.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed a race on DX12 where graphics work could change the state of a resource that async compute work was still using.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed a random crash on Windows when using the Direct3D12 graphics API, caused by a use-after-free when releasing GPU buffers.
    ([UUM-150682](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150682))

- DX12: Fixed an editor CPU performance regression in passes using DrawNullGeometry \(fullscreen blits, post-processing, VFX quads\) caused by running shader reflection on every draw call.
    (UUM-144068)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where the Editor leaked descriptor memory on every window repaint when using DirectX 12, which could crash long Editor sessions.
    (UUM-159625)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where VRAM used by small textures wasn't released back to the GPU driver after the textures were destroyed when using the Direct3D12 graphics API on Windows.<br>
    You can now tune the small-texture heap size with the `d3d12-small-texture-tiles-per-heap` boot config parameter to balance VRAM reclamation against heap-allocation overhead.
    ([UUM-142284](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142284))

- DX12: Fixed async compute command allocators on DX12 sometimes being reused while their GPU work was still executing.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed D3D12 async compute command buffers on the same queue reading a resource in the wrong state when a later command buffer in the same frame transitions it.
    (UUM-156477)

- DX12: Fixed errors being thrown when an empty texture is displayed in the Editor while using the Direct3D12 graphics API.
    ([UUM-144444](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144444))

- DX12: Fixed graphics API validation errors \(invalid resource state 9:538 and resource barrier state mismatch 8:527\) on depth-stencil attachments when native graphics jobs use split threading.
    (UUM-154103)

- DX12: Fixed IUnityGraphicsD3D12::GetNextFrameFenceValue\(\) returning a fence value that could complete before the commands a native plugin recorded into Unity's current command list when graphics jobs are enabled.
    (UUM-156768)

- DX12: Fixed missing depth testing in render pass subpasses that bind depth as read-only and read it as an input attachment when graphics jobs are enabled on DX12.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed Player Settings showing unusable D3D12 device filtering settings for platforms that don't support Direct3D12.
    (UUM-149251)

- DX12: Fixed random crash in RenderSurfaceD3D12::Reset.
    (UUM-142634)

- DX12: Fixed RenderTargetFlags.ReadOnlyDepth and RenderTargetFlags.ReadOnlyStencil being ignored on DX12 when not using native render passes, which caused incorrect resource states when sampling the bound depth texture.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Handle out of memory on very large texture creations.
    ([UUM-157106](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157106))

- DX12: Hardware Dynamic Resolution on D3D12 no longer produces a bright-pixel corruption artifact on AMD GPUs caused by uninitialised placed-resource compression metadata after a resolution-scale switch.
    ([UUM-108671](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/visual-artifacts-appear-when-the-hd-dynamic-resolution-component-changes-the-resolution-scale))

- DX12: HDRP Sample projects no longer spam D3D12 validation error \(category 9, id 1422\) on startup.
    (UUM-133610)

- DX12: Placed-resource aliasing on D3D12 no longer emits repeated D3D12 validation errors \(category 9, id 1422\) during Hardware DRS operation.
    (UUM-143015)

- DX12: Start tracking d3d12 resources using D3D12Object class on remaining D3D12 platforms similarly to how desktop does this.
    (UUM-147469)

- DX12: The Editor no longer crashes with an out-of-memory failure or a GPU device reset when a single repaint renders a scene with extreme per-frame draw volume. Direct3D 12 now submits GPU work mid-frame once more than 1 GiB of upload scratch memory has accumulated; the threshold can be changed, or set to 0 to disable it, with the d3d12-scratch-flush-threshold boot config parameter. The first time a mid-frame flush runs, the Editor or Player log names that parameter.
    (UUM-93272)

- Editor: Add duospace tag support to ATG
    ([UUM-146098](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146098))

- Editor: Added a developer-mode project setting to toggle persistent constant buffers for global shader variables.

- Editor: Added a Help button to the Build Analysis window.
    (UUM-147961)

- Editor: Added a reconnect handler for fast Play mode and RenderGraph viewer.
    ([UUM-147574](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147574))

- Editor: Added a static function CleanupRenderPipelineIfActive to prevent activeRenderPipelineDisposed from firing while disposing non-active render pipelines.
    ([UUM-148022](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148022))

- Editor: Added analyzer rules UAC1019 and UAC1020 \(both Error severity\) to detect \[SerializeReference\] fields in serialized dictionary keys and values, which cause silent data loss or dictionary corruption. The analyzer reports an error on the dictionary field declaration at compile time.
    (UUM-146298)

- Editor: Added directional-light Core tests.

- Editor: Added explicit padding in DrawCommandSettings to avoid garbage data when calculating hash value.
    ([UUM-144036](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/entities-graphics-entities-with-mesh-renderer-flicker-while-the-subscene-is-closed-when-batch-creating-a-large-amount-of-entities))

- Editor: Added group aware localization APIs.

- Editor: Added headless NativeJobs \(no container\) \[manually triggered\].

- Editor: Added headless Runtime/RegressionRuntime jobs \(no container\) \[manually triggered\].

- Editor: Added inspector warning box when layout element has conflicting min and max values.
    ([UUM-144431](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144431))

- Editor: Added some valid search terms/keywords for Indexing settings in "Preferences" window.
    ([UUM-146350](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146350))

- Editor: Added vulkan-specific issue where certain UITK elements with overflow:hidden would not render.
    ([UUM-142280](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142280))

- Editor: An issue where installing the Multiplayer Play Mode package did not enable the feature until the Editor was restarted.
    ([UUM-144481](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/default-play-mode-button-does-not-appear-in-the-editor-toolbar-after-installing-multiplayer-play-mode-package))

- Editor: Applications no longer hang when calling `SplashScreen.Stop(FadeOut)` while the splash screen is disabled.
    (UUM-143481)

- Editor: Asset Import Workers no longer crash and Player builds no longer fail when building after deleting and recreating an active Build Profile.
    ([UUM-141610](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/build-fails-and-asset-importers-crash-or-player-crashes-when-building-player-without-graphics-api-in-player-apis-list-after-recreating-build-profile))

- Editor: Awaitable.NextFrameAsync now resumes in edit mode, including when the Editor runs in batch mode, instead of waiting indefinitely for an unrelated scene update.
    (UUM-148723)

- Editor: Build Analysis JSON remove duplicated messages.
    (UUM-145857)

- Editor: Build Analysis UI is slow when large build selected.
    (UUM-143142)

- Editor: Build Profile Add Settings button now properly checks all providers when toggling enabled / disabled state.
    (UUM-144522)

- Editor: Changed max size of grid layout group to infinite. Restores previous expected behaviour.
    ([UUM-158101](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158101))

- Editor: Changed to float enums in sub graphs should update subgraph nodes properly now.
    ([UUM-143534](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sub-graphs-enum-float-entries-are-not-updated-on-the-main-shader-graph))

- Editor: Changing the size of an array through the inspector will now validate the requested array size against the max allowed object size which is 4GB. Element count and element size plus the remaining object data it taken into account.
    ([UUM-134407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134407))

- Editor: Codesigned several previously unsigned executables shipped with the Windows editor that could sometimes be blocked by antivirus software.
    (UUM-147382)

- Editor: Color space sync deferred until after initial asset DB refresh to avoid crash on startup.
    ([UUM-141297](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/an-refreshinternalv2-is-called-before-initialrefresh-error-is-thrown-when-opening-a-project-where-a-build-profile-with-player-settings-added-is-selected))

- Editor: Declaring an input component in a namespace that has the same name of its containing assembly, or declaring it when there's another namespace with a duplicated name no longer causes a compilation error. \(MTT-13332\).

- Editor: Deleting the script of a node or state created with the GraphToolkit public API now correctly shows its placeholder and the error associated with it.

- Editor: Disabled advanced font features for UITK editor text.
    ([UUM-147689](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147689))

- Editor: Disabled Open Frame Debugger from the UITK profiler Toolbar when running a standalone profiler.
    ([UUM-144370](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/frame-debugger-cannot-be-enabled-when-opened-from-ui-toolkit-details-module-of-standalone-profiler))

- Editor: Disabled Remove buttons in Build Profile settings during Play mode.
    ([UUM-143194](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/adaptive-performance-settings-in-the-build-profile-window-can-be-removed-when-in-play-mode-which-results-in-serializedobjectnotcreatableexception-errors-thrown))

- Editor: Don't optimize the projection matrix when Vulkan pre-transform may rotate it.
    ([UUM-150650](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150650))

- Editor: Driven RectTransform properties are read-only on a Prefab asset in the project window, matching in a Prefab instance.
    ([UUM-135352](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135352))

- Editor: Ensure SpriteRenderer material are indexed.
    (UUM-144279)

- Editor: Ensure the SearchDetailView actually refresh its uitk controls only when needed.
    ([UUM-137445](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137445))

- Editor: FilterFunctionDefinition asset's Inspector shows raw serialized fields instead of a proper UI Toolkit inspector.
    (UUM-148830)

- Editor: Fix to sort TreeView on query completion.
    (UUM-146365)

- Editor: Fixed a bug where a GameObject's cut styling persisted in the Hierarchy after entering Play Mode while paste command was no longer available.
    ([UUM-134218](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hierarchy-v2-node-action-state-not-properly-preserved-slash-cleared-during-playmode-transitions))

- Editor: Fixed a bug where clicking "Revert" in the prompt that appears when a build profile loses focus after adding a scripting define value in the scripting defines settings list does NOT result in removing the newly added value.
    ([UUM-147113](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147113))

- Editor: Fixed a bug where the Linux Editor wasn't anchoring the Help and Window menus to the end of the main menu bar.
    (UUM-113169)

- Editor: FIxed a bug where the main toolbar unity logo appeared vertically stretched on mac retina display.
    ([UUM-134520](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134520))

- Editor: Fixed a bug where the prefab stage header text did not update after the asset name was modified in the project browser.
    ([UUM-148172](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148172))

- Editor: Fixed a bug where Visual Element nodes with no name were not able to be renamed via double-click in the hierarchy.
    ([UUM-143826](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143826))

- Editor: Fixed a camera with Target Eye set to None rendering with the XR eye texture's size and aspect ratio instead of the window's while an XR display is active.
    ([UUM-152503](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152503))

- Editor: Fixed a crash \(ProfilerMutexLock / assertion gPersistentManager \!= NULL\) that occurred when a managed DomainUnload handler performed a UnityEngine.Object null-check during editor shutdown.
    ([UUM-144371](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-profilermutexlock-when-closing-a-project-with-specific-assets))

- Editor: Fixed a crash in ATG when using fallbacks whose Source Font File is missing.
    (UUM-148158)

- Editor: Fixed a crash on startup when the Unity Editor Software Terms had not been accepted and the terms file was missing, empty, or could not be read.
    ([UUM-154911](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154911))

- Editor: Fixed a crash that occurred when using multiline ellipsis with ATG.
    (UUM-147865)

- Editor: Fixed a crash that would occur when switching a FontAsset from static to Dynamic in ATG.
    ([UUM-144549](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/windows-editor-freezes-and-consumes-all-available-disk-space-when-changing-a-referenced-font-assets-atlas-population-mode-from-static-to-dynamic))

- Editor: Fixed a crash that would sometimes happening when opening the gizmo menu in the scene view.
    ([UUM-144488](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-annotationmanager-canhidelodlabels-when-opening-the-gizmos-dropdown-menu))

- Editor: Fixed a crash when a scene file contains a Prefab instance with malformed added components list.
    ([UUM-145507](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-deleteprefabinstanceobjectsformissingprefabs-when-opening-a-specific-scene))

- Editor: Fixed a crash when importing a Model with an empty or short `bodyMask` in clips data.
    ([UUM-144179](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-removedmaskedcurve-when-opening-a-project-with-specific-animations))

- Editor: Fixed a Hierarchy GameObject losing expanded state when another window is maximized and restored.
    ([UUM-146763](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146763))

- Editor: Fixed a Linux-only Editor crash caused by reentrancy while rebuilding menu items during a domain reload.
    (UUM-149839)

- Editor: Fixed a memory leak caused by an undisposed `CancellationTokenSource` object in `AsyncInstantiateOperation`.
    ([UUM-147016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147016))

- Editor: Fixed a MissingReferenceException that the Inspector logged on Play mode exit when it was scrolled down and showed a Volume component.
    ([UUM-153587](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153587))

- Editor: Fixed a NullReferenceException in `SamplePostprocessor.OnPostprocessAllAssets` that could sometimes occur when creating a Timeline asset.
    ([UUM-149739](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149739))

- Editor: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when selecting a GameObject in the Hierarchy while the Tool Contexts overlay was displayed but collapsed.
    ([UUM-141529](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141529))

- Editor: Fixed a tooltip in the Render Pipeline Converter that showed the word "package" twice for the disabled Post-Processing Stack v2 converter item when the Post Processing package is not installed.
    ([UUM-151386](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151386))

- Editor: Fixed a typo in the `services-gateway` fallback URL that could cause sign-in and Cloud services to fail on Editors launched via Hub when the remote config CDN was unreachable.
    (UUM-149207)

- Editor: Fixed a `NullReferenceException` error in the Console when extracting a variable to global scope in UI Builder.
    ([UUM-139826](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139826))

- Editor: Fixed account dropdown display when entering play mode.
    ([UUM-150759](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150759))

    ([UUM-143194](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/adaptive-performance-settings-in-the-build-profile-window-can-be-removed-when-in-play-mode-which-results-in-serializedobjectnotcreatableexception-errors-thrown))

- Editor: Fixed an ANR that occurred when a GameActivity based application was destroyed while paused.
    (UUM-147270)

- Editor: Fixed an attempt to delete a non-existent asset during a copy operation.
    ([UUM-144993](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144993))

- Editor: Fixed an Editor crash that occured when inspecting a TextMeshPro font asset.
    ([UUM-145753](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145753))

- Editor: Fixed an editor crash when a curve with more than 8 keys was set in a Particle System.
    ([UUM-144792](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144792))

- Editor: Fixed an incorrect max width calculation and propagation in layout groups when there are no child elements.
    ([UUM-152498](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152498))

- Editor: Fixed an intermittent issue with console window appearing unexpectedly during WinEditor launch.
    (UUM-150370)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where accessing the rootVisualElement of an Overlay's container could cause recursive calls and crash the Editor.
    ([UUM-149209](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149209))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where BC7E could rarely produce inconsistent texture compression results between different platforms / architectures.
    (UUM-135825)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where build failures during a pre-build callback weren't getting reported.
    (UUM-147054)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where build usage read and write errors navigated to the obsolete Project Auditor UI.
    (UUM-146279)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where building a player could fail with "Error building player because script class layout is incompatible between the editor and the player" for fields typed with BCL types \(for example pointers or HashSet fields in built-in packages\), depending on the active build target and API compatibility level.
    ([UUM-146005](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146005))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where changing the type of a connected Expression node in Shader Graph threw an `ArgumentException`.
    ([UUM-143151](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentexception-an-item-with-the-same-key-has-already-been-added-is-thrown-when-changing-expression-nodes-type-and-its-connected-to-a-node))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where clicking the Gradient settings button was ignored while editing a gradient preset's name.
    ([UUM-148458](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148458))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where commands weren't working in the Search Tree View.
    (UUM-149338)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where compressing textures to the ASTC format could be indeterministic on the same machine.
    (UUM-144035)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Control+Shift+Click in the **Hierarchy** window only selected a single item.
    ([UUM-141451](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141451))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where creating a build profile that installs packages, such as Android XR, could set up the profile before its packages finished importing, which left its quality level without a render pipeline asset.
    (UUM-156303)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where disabling the mimalloc memory allocator for the Windows standalone player did not work.
    (UUM-147576)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where editing a removed binding produced errors. The binding window now closes if it's editing a binding that is removed.
    ([UUM-147201](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147201))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where EditorSceneManager.SaveOpenScenes return true despite open scenes are not saved due to being blocked in ShouldSaveScenesAndAssets.
    ([UUM-142487](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editorscenemanager-dot-saveopenscenes-returns-true-when-the-scene-is-not-successfully-saved))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where entering Play Mode, using Search, or opening the Inspector logged a false "Cross scene references are not supported" warning for objects that weren't being saved.
    ([UUM-146260](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146260))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where ListView controls, such as the Hierarchy window, did not scroll with the mouse wheel during a drag-and-drop operation in the Windows Editor.
    (UUM-132932)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where MainToolbar.Refresh could cause an infinite recursion in the code.
    (STO-3778)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor showed the Optimization page and highlighted the wrong sidebar entry after starting an analysis from the Migrate to URP tab.
    (UUM-155263)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where refreshing an empty Project Auditor view switched it to a flat list and turned off Show Ignored Issues.
    (UUM-156115)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where scripts were not being created after pressing **Enter** in the INspector's Add Component New Script view.
    ([UUM-149682](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149682))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where selecting multiple ranges of items in the **Hierarchy** window wasn't possible.
    ([UUM-142974](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142974))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Shift+clicking a prefab child in the Scene view selected the child object instead of the prefab instance root.
    ([UUM-148884](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148884))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the 2D Tile Pallet window closed immediately after opening on the Linux Editor.
    ([UUM-146416](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146416))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the Cameras overlay did not list or select the Prefab's camera in Prefab Mode.
    ([UUM-156200](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156200))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the Code and Shaders Compiler Messages views in Project Auditor overwrote each other's saved column widths, grouping, and sorting.
    (UUM-156118)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the Hierarchy selection highlight would disappear and global selection would not update when right-clicking on an empty space in the Hierarchy window.
    ([UUM-145820](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gameobject-in-the-hierarchy-loses-visual-selection-even-though-the-gameobject-is-still-selected-when-right-clicking-the-hierarchy))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the Hierarchy window logged warnings to the Console window when entering Play mode while the window was open but not visible.
    ([UUM-146302](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146302))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the Inspector window jittered up and down when hovering over a GameObject with a new Material in the Scene view.
    ([UUM-147340](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147340))

- Editor: Fixed blue focus highlight sometimes doesn't disappear when unfocusing the messages in the Build Analysis.
    ([UUM-143897](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143897))

    ([UUM-149532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149532))

- Editor: Fixed bug where `InstantiateAsync` would not account for parent transform scale when `Instantiate` would.

- Editor: Fixed built-in Lighting Search query names to be title-cased, so authored names like "1. &lt; 10 lumen \(Low\)" now display as written.

    ([UUM-144458](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144458))

- Editor: Fixed case of inspector not updating when navigating the Hierarchy V2 with Left Arrow key.
    ([UUM-146977](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146977))

- Editor: Fixed case of mesh particles rendering with incorrect UVs \(and other vertex channels\) when the source mesh used a non-float vertex format such as 8-bit UNorm.
    ([UUM-141864](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141864))

- Editor: Fixed case of PolySpatial network connections failing to shut down cleanly, which could spin a reader thread at 100% CPU until teardown.

- Editor: Fixed case of Project Auditor URP rules \(URP0002, URP0003, URP0101, URP0102, URP0103, URP0201, URP0401\) incorrectly reporting Error severity for non-compile-error issues; they now report Major severity.

- Editor: Fixed case of Project Auditor's `Audit()` API returning a successful, empty report instead of throwing an error when the Project Auditor Rules package is not installed.
    ([UUM-151218](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151218))

- Editor: Fixed case of SelectAll/DeselectAll button in platform discovery window not working when package count exceeds scroll view.

- Editor: Fixed case of the Hierarchy window Tag column not updating when a newly created Tag is assigned to multiple selected GameObjects from the Inspector.
    ([UUM-134208](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134208))

- Editor: Show in Project \(the row context menu and the inspector Select button\) silently did nothing for renamed/deleted assets. Both entry points now disable the action when the asset can no longer be resolved, and surface a non-modal "Asset not found" notification if it is triggered otherwise.
    ([UUM-143853](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143853))

- Editor: Skip post-build audit when Project Auditor Rules package isn't installed.
    ([UUM-152518](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152518))

- Editor: SpriteMask not rendering on Android, iOS, and Web when "Strip Engine Code" is enabled and the Tilemap package is disabled in Package Manager'.
    ([UUM-138419](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138419))

- Editor: Stop missing animation trigger warnings being logged on every transition, on every keystroke of invalid transition names.
    ([UUM-144071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144071))

- Editor: Support for the SpeedTreeWindAsset created in the 2022 Editor.
    ([UUM-147077](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147077))

- Editor: The Build Analysis window's Assets tab now shows the asset breakdown for scripts-only and incremental Player builds by attributing them to the source build whose content they reused \(previously empty for these builds\).
    ([UUM-142982](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142982))

- Editor: The collapse repeat-count badge inherited the white selection-text color, making the count invisible on its light pill in the Light theme. The badge now sets its own theme text color.
    ([UUM-143790](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143790))

- Editor: The headless Linux Editor cpu core count computation when run within a container that has CPU resource limits. Affinity mask and Cgroup settings will now be taken into account. Note that changing the affinity while the Editor is running will not have any affect on its CPU usage as the value is cached at application startup.
    (UUM-145740)

- Editor: The Overview Log column showed the entire multi-paragraph message as an auto-tooltip. The tooltip has been removed; the full message is shown in the detail pane when a row is selected.
    ([UUM-143232](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143232))

- Editor: This PR fixed a bug where reordering GameObjects showed the "+" \(add\) icon instead of the arrow icon by mappping DragVisualMode.Move to DragAndDropVisualMode.Link.
    ([UUM-115073](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-115073))

- Editor: Turn off GRD in URP and HDRP templates.
    ([UUM-143179](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143179))

- Editor: Universal Render Pipeline: Fixed outdated asset importer warnings when creating a project from the URP 3D Sample template.
    ([UUM-146261](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146261))

- Editor: Updated Content Directories to take Application.backgroundLoadingPriority into account when loading.
    (UUM-154823)

- Editor: Updated included VCRedist to 14.51.36247 for the Windows Editor.
    (UUM-156578)

- Editor: Updated Stats window to use M suffix for triangle and vertex counts 1 million or greater.
    ([UUM-146878](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146878))

- Editor: Updated the dialog that appears when Unity detects scene backups from an Editor session that did not close correctly. The dialog is now titled Scene Backup Detected, and its Keep Backups and Delete buttons state whether the backups are kept in Assets/_Recovery/ or discarded.
    (UUM-147629)

- Editor: Updated WinEditor's warning dialog text when running Unity with elevated privileges.
    ([UUM-146796](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146796))

- Editor: Updating package while an MPPM clone is active causes the clone to crash.
    (UUM-148292)

- Editor: USS files that import a built-in theme are now reimported automatically when the referenced theme changes.

- Editor: When building a Web player with Progressive Asset Loading using the build scripting API, the build will fail \(with a helpful error message\) if incompatible settings are enabled.

- Editor: When graphics jobs is enabled the Editor now draw renderers whose shader variant compiled during the frame. Asset previews and one-shot renders which were previously blank due to a missing shader should now draw as expected via the SRP Batcher fallback path.
    (UUM-147205)

- Editor: Windows: Fixed a crash on Windows when the Editor ran in batch mode and could not create its main window.
    ([UUM-153339](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153339))

- Editor: XR: Removed the obsolete and unused AR module, the `UnityPlayer.initializeGoogleAr()` method, and the use of the `unity.arcore-enable` manifest metadata key.

- Editor: \[Android\] Display an error informing users of the reason a device went offline during apk installation on Unity 6.7.
    (UUM-151550)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed a crash when no `wl_seat` is present on the system.
    (UUM-148754)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed case where first touch input is dropped.
    (UUM-150086)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed empty publish section being shown.
    (UUM-144616)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed for Screen.SetResolution produces a blank screen when used with FullScreenMode.MaximizedWindow.
    (UUM-143541)

- Engine Diagnostics: Crash, exception and ANR reports captured before remote configuration now keep their essential attributes, including the session they occurred in.

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed a possible crash when a crash report was logged from a worker thread while the Engine Diagnostics configuration was being applied.
    (UUM-158113)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed managed stack frames in IL2CPP crash reports showing no file name or line number when Cloud Diagnostics crash reporting is enabled in non-development builds.
    (UUM-155430)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed performance metrics being reported without a sum, which prevented average values from being calculated.
    (UUM-155716)

- Entities: Fixed a bug where a scene's GameObjects would disappear from the Hierarchy window after exiting Play mode when a SubScene referenced the same scene.
    ([UUM-148713](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148713))

- Entities: Fixed a memory leak in the EntityNameStorage by properly disposing NameStoreAccess.
    ([UUM-147280](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147280))

- Entities: Fixed a race condition in EntityId allocation pool initialization. This was likely to happen when loading a scene containing multiple SubScenes in the editor.
    (UUM-147566)

- Entities: Fixed a `NullReferenceException` that occurred when pressing the "+" button in the Systems window search field with "Show All Worlds" enabled during Play Mode.
    ([UUM-146668](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146668))

- Entities: Fixed an ArgumentNullException during Sub Scene import after clearing the Scene Asset of a Sub Scene.

- Entities: Fixed an assertion failure and duplicate SubScene registration that occurred on domain reload in scenes containing multiple SubScenes.
    (UUM-147566)

- Entities: Fixed an editor crash \(SIGSEGV\) that occurred when dragging a SubScene into another SubScene that references the same scene asset.
    ([UUM-142972](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-crashes-with-sigsegv-when-dragging-a-sub-scene-into-a-duplicated-sub-scene))

- Entities: Fixed an error "Failed to find Entity child node" in the New Hierarchy when removing child status from a GameObject that was added to an existing parent inside a Sub Scene.
    ([UUM-146762](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146762))

- Entities: Fixed an exception in the Hierarchy window when setting "Type of Worlds Shown" to include all world types.
    ([UUM-146725](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146725))

- Entities: Fixed an InvalidOperationException \("does not exist in the hierarchy"\) being logged repeatedly in the console after switching away from a scene with an open Sub Scene and switching back.

- Entities: Fixed an InvalidOperationException when searching by component type in the Hierarchy window while a SubScene is loading.
    ([UUM-142749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142749))

- Entities: Fixed an issue in the Systems window where the toolbar and search bar would shrink when viewing a large number of systems.
    (UUM-146060)

- Entities: Fixed an issue where editing a read-only nested text field in the Entity Buffer Inspector threw an `AccessViolationException`. The read-only field is now properly disabled.
    ([UUM-148905](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148905))

- Entities: Fixed an issue where rebuilding an unchanged project produced different content archives, causing published remote content to be downloaded again.
    ([UUM-151571](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151571))

- Entities: Fixed an issue where renaming a subscene asset in the Project view would not update the subscene name displayed in the Hierarchy window.
    (UUM-143368)

- Entities: Fixed an issue where the authoring representation of an object would be shown instead of the runtime representation, when selecting the object through Entities Hierarchy.
    ([UUM-152665](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152665))

- Entities: Fixed an issue where `WeakReference` fields showed "None" when assigned by GUID from code.
    ([UUM-134270](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134270))

- Entities: Fixed an outdated deprecation warning in the Entities Hierarchy window that incorrectly referenced a Preferences toggle that no longer exists.
    ([UUM-146983](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146983))

- Entities: Fixed an `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` thrown when clicking items in the Systems window after domain reload.
    ([UUM-148748](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148748))

- Entities: Fixed an `ObjectDisposedException` and leaked world nodes in the Entities Hierarchy when a world is disposed.
    (UUM-146293)

- Entities: Fixed and re-enable unstable System window search tests.
    (UUM-147320)

- Entities: Fixed case of project build failing with "IL2CPP error C2039: __entity is not a member of BlobBuilder" error.
    ([UUM-86534](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-86534))

- Entities: Fixed clear \("x"\) button in Entities search bars remaining visible after deleting all query blocks via right-click.
    ([UUM-146465](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146465))

- Entities: Fixed Entities Memory Profiler showing duplicate components when multiple worlds share the same archetype.

- Entities: Fixed Entities, Worlds, and SubScenes appearing in the Hierarchy window when editing UI Toolkit assets.

- Entities: Fixed GameObjects in an open SubScene disappearing from the Hierarchy window after the SubScene GameObject was disabled and enabled.
    ([UUM-154731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154731))

    ([UUM-145910](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145910))

- Entities: Fixed Ignoring invalid \[UpdateAfterAttribute\] warning on the ExplicitFilterSystem when disposing the Hierarchy World Handler.
    ([UUM-146672](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146672))

    ([UUM-148849](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148849))

    ([UUM-146747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146747))

- Entities: Fixed performance regressions related to transform hierarchy children access.
    (UUM-138633)

- Entities: Fixed stale entity subscene nodes in the new Hierarchy when using the AutoLoadScene property on subscenes.
    ([UUM-143057](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143057))

- Entities: Fixed subscene reimport so that it no longer creates entities that are never removed.
    ([UUM-148293](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148293))

- Entities: Fixed the entity list foldout under a SubScene in the Hierarchy collapsing after an undo of reordering or reparenting SubScenes.
    ([UUM-147837](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147837))

- Entities: Fixed the SubScene foldout icon disappearing in the Hierarchy window after undoing changes to the SubScene GameObject.

- Entities: Fixed the Systems window world dropdown to exclude internal world types that could cause an ArgumentException when selected.
    ([UUM-148241](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148241))

- Entities: Fixed unsaved changes in a Sub Scene being lost when changing its Scene Asset and pressing Cancel in the save prompt.

- Entities: Fixed unsaved changes in a Sub Scene being lost without a save prompt when a duplicate Sub Scene that references the same scene was deleted or had its Scene Asset changed.
    ([UUM-154590](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154590))

- Entities: In the editor, every subscene load used to leak a duplicate of each loaded GameObject until domain reload; only the intended instances are now created.
    (UUM-145926)

- Entities: Made the EntityId hash its full 64-bits value, reduces the risk of collisions in hash containers.
    (UUM-145687)

- Entities: NullReferenceException in AsyncLoadSceneOperation.ScheduleSceneRead\(\) when loading subscenes containing UnityObjectRef with invalid EntityIds.
    (UUM-146245)

- Entities: Only record user-initiated selection in Systems window navigation history.
    (UUM-150002)

- Entities: Overwrite duplicate keys in the case TypeManager.AllTypes yields multiple entries with the same FullName, preventing an exception.
    ([UUM-147907](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147907))

- Entities: Removed per frame GC Allocations in ComponentSystemGroup.
    (UUM-151683)

- Entities: StableTypeHash of ECS types were inconsistent between Mono and CoreCLR.

- Entities: Systems window "Show Full Player Loop" dropdown now works correctly. The setting has been split into two independent toggles: "Show Player Loop" \(controls visibility of Unity player loop subsystems\) and "Show All Worlds" \(controls world filtering\).
    ([UUM-138413](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/entities-systems-window-has-a-show-full-player-loop-dropdown-which-does-nothing-when-clicked-after-enabling-show-full-player-loop))

- Entities: Systems window "Show Full Player Loop" should be disabled by default.
    (UUM-146280)

- Entities Graphics: Fixed baked directional lights authored inside Entities subscenes not driving HDRP's Physically Based Sky.
    ([UUM-137400](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137400))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed corrupted compute-deformation of skinned meshes in subscenes.
    ([UUM-146932](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146932))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed instances in a subscene not rendering in the Player after another subscene that uses the same meshes and materials was unloaded.
    ([UUM-124966](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-124966))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed Physically Based Sky rendering incorrectly in HDRP for directional lights authored inside Entities subscenes.
    ([UUM-137400](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137400))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed static entities not updating lighting when light probes change and the entity does not.
    ([UUM-151337](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151337))

- GI: A Terrain added to a ray-tracing acceleration structure no longer leaks a Mesh and its GPU buffers.
    (UUM-151903)

- GI: Added a Screen Space Reflections \(SSR\) renderer feature to URP.

- GI: Added Enlighten realtime GI deprecation warning to lighting window and lighting settings inspector.

- GI: Changed the default lightmap denoiser from Optix to OpenImage in Advanced filtering mode.
    ([UUM-142567](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/optix-denoiser-is-set-as-default-in-streams-where-it-should-be-obsolete-when-using-custom-lightmap-filtering))

- GI: Disabled surface cache GI in material previews and thumbnails.

- GI: Fix to respect double sided gi / render face flags in surface cache GI.

- GI: Fixed "Kernel 'histogram' not found" errors and the lightmap scheduling fallback warning that occurred when viewing lightmaps in preview mode with the Editor running on a graphics API without the histogram compute shader variant, such as Vulkan.
    ([UUM-151644](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151644))

- GI: Fixed "Unknown filter" logs in Lighting Search saved queries.
    ([UUM-147133](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147133))

- GI: Fixed a bug where "Refresh Virtual Offset Debug" throws an exception if the last bake didn't have Virtual Offset enabled.
    ([UUM-147479](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147479))

- GI: Fixed a bug where APV bake fails when scene contains a VFXRenderer.
    ([UUM-150965](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150965))

- GI: Fixed a bug where mixed lights appear fully occluded when using APV and having L2 spherical harmonics disabled.
    ([UUM-156430](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156430))

- GI: Fixed a bug where reflection probes sometimes would not be re-baked when baking lighting, if only URP-specific settings were modified.
    ([UUM-133949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133949))

- GI: Fixed a crash that occurred when extracting shadow casters with null material.
    ([UUM-132304](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132304))

- GI: Fixed a crash when baking Reflection Probes after baking Adaptive Probe Volumes.
    ([UUM-143885](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-pptr-operator-when-baking-reflection-probes-after-baking-probe-volumes-multiple-times-with-specific-lighting-data))

- GI: Fixed a crash when generating lighting for an object with a transparent material that uses an unreadable texture, such as a TextMesh Pro font material.
    ([UUM-148232](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148232))

- GI: Fixed a crash when opening the Profiler \(Standalone Process\) window in projects created from templates that include the Tutorials framework.
    ([UUM-149676](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149676))

- GI: Fixed a heap-buffer-overflow read when deduplicating overlapping Light Probes from multiple loaded scenes.
    (UUM-153133)

- GI: Fixed a warning that appeared when canceling an Adaptive Probe Volume \(APV\) bake.
    (UUM-146305)

- GI: Fixed Adaptive Probe Volumes baking black probes near terrain surfaces when using Vulkan on some AMD GPUs.
    (UUM-157162)

- GI: Fixed Adaptive Probe Volumes placing probes as if the scene had no geometry when baking with Vulkan on AMD GPUs.
    ([UUM-137732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137732))

- GI: Fixed an "Unknown reflection probe mode" error when baking reflection probes in a scene with Realtime reflection probes.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Fixed an Editor crash when using the interactive lighting preview in a scene with a terrain large enough to be split into multiple lightmapping instances.
    (UUM-148850)

- GI: Fixed an gradient-in-loop shader warning that would occur in SSR bilateral upscale.
    ([UUM-144964](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gradient-instruction-used-in-a-loop-with-varying-iteration-at-declarenormalstexture-shader-warning-is-thrown-after-building-urp-template-project))

- GI: Fixed an issue where changing the Lighting Data Asset field in the Baked Lightmaps tab of the Lighting window could not be undone and did not appear in Undo History.
    ([UUM-133695](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133695))

- GI: Fixed an issue where Generate Lighting baked a cubemap for Realtime Reflection Probes.
    ([UUM-158749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158749))

- GI: Fixed an issue where selecting the currently set APV baking set in the Lighting / Adaptive Probe Volume / Current Baking Set property did not function.
    ([UUM-145389](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/apv-data-becomes-incorrect-when-reselecting-the-currently-active-baking-set))

- GI: Fixed an issue with punctual light sample index clamp in Surface Cache path tracing.
    ([UUM-147818](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147818))

- GI: Fixed case of the Static Batching message box in the Surface Cache Global Illumination Renderer Feature inspector not matching the icon size and text alignment of the other message boxes.

- GI: Fixed cone spot shadows missing with camera-relative culling.
    ([UUM-147026](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147026))

- GI: Fixed crash when baking lighting for a scene placed in StreamingAssets.
    ([UUM-149240](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149240))

- GI: Fixed Editor crashes when building a GPU BVH from an empty primitive span or with a maxLeafSize of 0.
    ([UUM-154597](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154597))

- GI: Fixed Global Illumination APV task left Not Responding on cancel.
    ([UUM-144776](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/global-illumination-task-becomes-not-responding-when-quickly-cancelling-adaptive-probe-volume-baking))

- GI: Fixed light baking failing when the only Global Illumination contributors in a scene had no materials assigned.
    (UUM-153676)

- GI: Fixed Lighting window "New" creating assets in immutable folders.
    ([UUM-147196](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147196))

- GI: Fixed MissingComponentException for MeshRenderers without a MeshFilter.

- GI: Fixed nullptr PPtr error changing reflection resolution with null skybox.
    (UUM-132378)

- GI: Fixed objects whose lightmap UVs collapse to a point or a line being removed from baked lighting with an "Instance with invalid UV bounds was removed" warning.
    (UUM-148851)

- GI: Fixed objects without valid lightmap UVs being removed from baked lighting with an "Every triangle in the Mesh UV layout has zero area" warning.
    ([UUM-148666](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148666))

- GI: Fixed shader compiler warnings shown in the Inspector when selecting the Adaptive Probe Volumes VoxelizeScene shader.
    ([UUM-153983](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153983))

- GI: Fixed speckles/white spots with APV.
    ([UUM-133626](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133626))

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache geometry rendering incorrectly after many meshes were added to the scene.

- GI: Fixed the custom environment reflection cubemap being forgotten when changing Source in the Lighting window.
    (UUM-133122)

- GI: Fixed the Scenes in Baking Set list in the Lighting window so that a scene can be added again after undoing its addition, and so that removing a scene can be undone.
    ([UUM-147477](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147477))

- GI: Fixed the Unity Compute Light Baker information box in Graphics settings not hiding when the Default Light Baker setting was reverted with Reset or Undo.
    ([UUM-151751](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151751))

- GI: Fixed the Windows Editor failing to start when the Microsoft Visual C++ 2010 runtime is not installed; the CPU Progressive Lightmapper now reports that it is unavailable instead.
    (UUM-149122)

- GI: Move SurfaceCache debug settings to the Rendering Debugger.

- GI: Refactored `MaterialGlobalIlluminationFlags` and emissive material settings to reflect how we expect the user to use the flags.
    ([UUM-134070](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-emission-keyword-removed-from-material-when-global-illumination-is-set-to-none))

- GI: Skip APV sampling when MeshRenderer.lightProbeUsage is Off in URP.
    ([UUM-101953](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/apv-meshrenderers-using-off-for-the-lightprobes-option-still-sample-apv))

- GI: When the Screen Space Reflection renderer feature in URP is not present on the active renderer asset, the SSR volume override now displays a warning InfoBox with an "Open" button that selects the active renderer asset.

- Graph Toolkit: A visualization context will now restore itself on window reopen.
    ([UUM-152695](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152695))

- Graph Toolkit: Changed orientation blackboard variable and node foldouts to face right instead of left.
    ([UUM-139425](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139425))

- Graph Toolkit: Expose Frame selection and Disconnect All Transitions shortcuts in the state's contextual menu.

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when setting the Title, Subtitle, Tooltip, DefaultColor or FillAmount of a Node created from script before setting its Position.
    ([UUM-150194](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150194))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where characters typed with an IME, such as Korean or Japanese, were duplicated in string fields on Graph Toolkit nodes.
    ([UUM-130669](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-130669))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where converting a List or Array variable node to a constant reset its values.
    (UUM-139307)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where short portal names were cut off because the portal node did not keep a minimum width.
    ([UUM-133912](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133912))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed bug where GraphLogger messages would be logged twice.
    ([UUM-151632](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151632))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed bug where opening a subgraph for the first time would not always frame its contents.
    ([UUM-131387](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/graph-view-is-not-centered-when-opening-a-local-subgraph-for-the-first-time))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed case of an error erroneously appearing on ports when `Graph.IsConnectionAllowed` was overridden to permit connections between incompatible types. `CheckNodeErrors` now delegates type validation to `CanAssignTo`, consistent with how connection creation already behaves.
    (UUM-150703)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed Copy/Paste of values in fields inside of nodes.
    ([UUM-151929](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151929))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed IPort.Name returning null for some variable node ports, which caused name-based IsConnectionAllowed rules to throw a NullReferenceException.
    ([UUM-151603](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151603))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed options and ports TrySetValue with Enum.
    ([UUM-146236](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146236))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the Graph Inspector's Narrow the Selection list showing internal type names such as VariableDeclarationBase instead of user-facing names such as Variable.
    ([UUM-154274](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154274))

- Graph Toolkit: Session.GraphView is now always resolved on access instead of fetching a cached copy. This fixes bugs related to accessing an outdated graph view through the session.
    ([UUM-151202](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151202))
