- Graph Toolkit: The collapse button of a node with options is now enabled even when all the ports are connected. This allows pressing the collapse button to collapse the node just like it was possible by choosing Toggle Collapse or using Shift+Ctrl+O.
    ([UUM-150163](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150163))

- Graphics: Added warning when the specified Keyword Declaration Override does not match any Keyword Declaration in the project.
    ([UUM-151445](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151445))

- Graphics: Added `IEquatable<T>`, `GetHashCode`, and equality operators to `Resolution` and `RefreshRate`, to avoid GC allocations when comparing them or using them as Dictionary keys.
    (UUM-146197)

- Graphics: Allow On Tile Post Processing feature only on the Universal Renderer.
    ([UUM-148038](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148038))

- Graphics: Changed to staticShadowCaster now update properly with GRD enabled.
    ([UUM-140724](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140724))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in DirectX12 Split Graphics Jobs when using RayTracing.
    (UUM-145956)

- Graphics: Fixed a crash on Vulkan when pre-warming pipeline state objects with GraphicsStateCollection.WarmUpProgressively.
    (UUM-154071)

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when a build started while occlusion culling was baking in the background.
    ([UUM-114053](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-114053))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when baking occlusion culling for large scenes.
    ([UUM-143361](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143361))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when calling Graphics.CreateGraphicsFence or CommandBuffer.CreateGraphicsFence with an undefined GraphicsFenceType value.
    ([UUM-151166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151166))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash with occlusion culling in scenes with a very large number of occludees.
    ([UUM-135920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135920))

- Graphics: Fixed a device freeze on Midgard Mali GPUs when Blit Type was set to Auto.
    (UUM-149414)

- Graphics: Fixed a GPU driver crash on ARM Mali-G68 devices caused by `log2` using relaxed precision in SPIR-V.
    ([UUM-137912](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137912))

- Graphics: Fixed a GPU hang on Metal that could occur when a compute shader requested a larger thread group than the graphics pipeline supports \(for example, High Quality Line Rendering on some Intel GPUs\).
    (UUM-147457)

- Graphics: Fixed a URP performance regression on OpenGL ES devices where the maximum number of additional lights was forced from 16 to 32.
    (UUM-148728)

- Graphics: Fixed a `NullReferenceException` thrown by `DynamicLightController` in the URP sample template Garden scene when the component's `Awake()` ran in the Editor before the runtime-only light manager list was initialized.
    ([UUM-145743](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/universal-3d-sample-nullreferenceexception-is-thrown-when-opening-gardenscene-in-urp-17-dot-3-0-sample))

- Graphics: Fixed an assert "Metal: memoryless texture requires 2D texture." that the Editor logged on Metal when it rendered single-pass XR to memoryless texture arrays.
    (UUM-154442)

- Graphics: Fixed an assertion for out-of-bounds memory access when GPU Resident Drawer instancing is enabled, Burst compilation is disabled, and a Mesh Renderer has more material slots than submeshes.
    ([UUM-147082](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147082))

- Graphics: Fixed an error "Cannot use Store Action Store in memoryless color attachment" that the Editor logged for memoryless render textures.
    (UUM-154195)

- Graphics: Fixed an InvalidOperationException when a script disposed the destination NativeArray of an in-flight AsyncGPUReadback request on exiting Play Mode.
    ([UUM-143136](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143136))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue that could cause memory leak warnings, primarily on macOS, while Unity processed shader jobs with GPU Resident Drawer enabled.
    ([UUM-140185](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140185))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where a Line Renderer or Trail Renderer using a 2D sprite material became invisible after turning off Preview in the Animation window or after calling Renderer.SetPropertyBlock.
    ([UUM-156620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156620))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where URP and HDRP did not upgrade materials when you imported a project with -batchmode -quit.
    ([UUM-153768](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153768))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where Vulkan Player would not respect the value of 'PlayerSettings.vulkanNumSwapchainBuffers' and instead use a fixed count of 3 swapchain buffers.
    (UUM-148724)

- Graphics: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when updating graphics buffers on Metal.
    (UUM-153132)

- Graphics: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when warming up shader variants on Metal.
    (UUM-153134)

- Graphics: Fixed AsyncGPUReadback.RequestIntoNativeArray logging read/write access and undisposable errors for a NativeArray that was used by a completed job.
    ([UUM-102552](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-102552))

- Graphics: Fixed crash in `RenderTarget::SetActive` when Asset import is triggered, and the camera's target buffers were set using `Camera.SetTargetBuffers`.
    ([UUM-148003](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148003))

- Graphics: Fixed crash when importing Texture2DArrays with small dimensions and many elements.
    ([UUM-153670](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153670))

- Graphics: Fixed Graphics.RenderPrimitives and Graphics.DrawProcedural crash with MeshTopology.Quads when Graphics Jobs are enabled.
    ([UUM-141117](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141117))

- Graphics: Fixed memory corruption when BC-compressed textures are decompressed on the CPU, such as when the GPU does not support the format, and a mip level is smaller than 4x4 or not a multiple of 4 in size.
    (UUM-154799)

- Graphics: Fixed misaligned warning icon in shader Inspector.
    ([UUM-154699](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154699))

- Graphics: Fixed missing objects and shadows in Quad Views with camera stacking.
    ([UUM-150107](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150107))

- Graphics: Fixed reflection probes intermittently missing in URP on some Vulkan Android devices.
    (UUM-155930)

- Graphics: Fixed rendering issues on XR when dynamic resolution is enabled with an injected RenderObjects pass.
    ([UUM-142592](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142592))

- Graphics: Fixed shader compilation failure while using UNITY_DISPLAY_ORIENTATION_PRETRANSFORM with Adreno devices.
    (UUM-145641)

- Graphics: Fixed SSAO rendering incorrectly in the right eye when using single-pass multiview XR \(Vulkan / OpenGL ES\).
    (UUM-142984)

- Graphics: Fixed SystemInfo.supportsConservativeRaster returning false and conservative rasterization being unavailable on Vulkan on GPUs that report a maxExtraPrimitiveOverestimationSize of 0, such as AMD GPUs.
    ([UUM-143199](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143199))

- Graphics: Fixed the Adaptive Probe Volume Samples fields accepting typed values above the slider maximum.
    ([UUM-148224](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148224))

- Graphics: Fixed the help box under "Preferred Graphics Jobs Filters" in the D3D12 Device Filter Inspector being too small and cutting off its text.
    ([UUM-134259](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134259))

- Graphics: Fixed the right eye in XR multi-pass selecting different LODs than the left eye when using camera stacking.
    (UUM-157818)

- Graphics: Fixed URP mixing MSAA sample counts for overlay UI textures.

- Graphics: Fixed URP reflection probes rendering black on some Vulkan Android devices.
    (UUM-154967)

- Graphics: Fixed Vulkan rendering artifacts on Mali GPUs with some renderpass/subpass configurations.
    ([UUM-147578](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147578))

- Graphics: Graphics Settings - "Import from current scene" action is not added to the undo list.
    ([UUM-148881](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148881))

- Graphics: Notify ObjectDispatcher when MeshRenderer LocalAABB changes.
    ([UUM-147452](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147452))

- Graphics: Quality Settings - Adding elipsis for default platform and Quality Level Header for quality levels with very long names.
    ([UUM-148889](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148889))

- Graphics: Quality Settings - Show confirmation dialog when RP asset changes betwen quality levels when changing current quality.
    (UUM-146325)

- Graphics: Resolved an issue when using Vulkan on Windows with Split Graphics Jobs where null could be inserted into a cache, resulting in DrawCalls trying to draw to a closed Command Buffer, causing a crash.
    (UUM-132307)

- Graphics: Shader Build Settings UI now shows error for invalid Shader Constant Defines.
    ([UUM-153573](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153573))

- Graphics: ShadowCaster objects using the Renderer Shader User Value now render correct shadows when the value changes at runtime. Previously the shadow caster reused the rendering layer value from the last non-shadow draw unless the SRP Batcher path was active.
    ([UUM-137149](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137149))

- Graphics: Texture2D.ReadPixels and ScreenCapture could return stale data on AMD GPUs with Vulkan when graphics jobs were enabled and the GPU was busy.
    (UUM-149529)

- Graphics: Unexpected failures in Vulkan swapchain are now correctly logged.
    ([UUM-153744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153744))

- Graphics: URP Decals shader could rarely produce NaN values on Android platforms when sampling non-normal-map textures set as the Normal Map, especially visible as white flashes when in conjunction with other amplifying effects like Bloom.
    ([UUM-153167](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153167))

- Graphics: Vulkan: Fixed a random crash caused by an invalid shader module when shaders were unloaded and reloaded with graphics jobs enabled.
    (UUM-136086)

- Graphics: \[Android\] Fixed frame pacing settling on an incorrect frame rate on variable refresh rate \(VRR\) displays.
    (UUM-95307)

- Graphics: \[Android\] Fixed the frame rate not matching the requested rate when `OnDemandRendering.renderFrameInterval` was greater than 1.
    ([UUM-68029](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-68029))

- Graphics: `Screen.currentBackbufferMSAASamples` on Android Vulkan now correctly returns the MSAA sample value of the back buffer.

- Graphics: `ScriptableRenderContext` draws that use only `SortingCriteria.OptimizeStateChanges` no longer produce more SRP batches than `SortingCriteria.RenderPriority` for scenes that mix `MeshRenderer` and `SkinnedMeshRenderer` objects sharing a material. Previously the sort split these renderers into separate groups by renderer type, breaking SRP batches that the batcher would otherwise coalesce.
    ([UUM-141053](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141053))

- GraphView: Added issue in Template Browser where the tab keyboard shortcut sometimes caused keyboard navigation to go out of sync with selections.
    ([UUM-144972](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144972))

- GraphView: Allowed multiple instances of the Template Browser to coexist for different tools.
    (UUM-137579)

- GraphView: Changing the Template Browser Sort Type preserves selection.
    ([UUM-143043](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143043))

- GraphView: Ensure selection is visible when changing the Sort Type for the Template Browser.
    ([UUM-143045](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143045))

- GraphView: Fixed the Template Browser displaying errors when creating an asset from a deleted template.
    ([UUM-143138](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143138))

- GraphView: Handle exceptions from various keyboard commands when used in the Template Browser.
    ([UUM-144108](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144108))

- GraphView: Improved responsiveness of search index status banner.
    ([UUM-150720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150720))

- GraphView: Scroll to last template now in the Template Browser now accounts for collapsed categories.
    ([UUM-140556](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140556))

- GraphView: Search focus in the Template Browser is now preserved after the first match.
    ([UUM-144963](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144963))

- GraphView: Tab navigation now fully ignores the list view.
    ([UUM-150861](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150861))

- HDRP: Added missing `UnityPerDraw` params for `SpriteRenderer`.
    (UUM-141304)

- HDRP: Ensure global settings default volume profile in difussion profile editor.
    ([UUM-138525](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138525))

- HDRP: Fixed "Default" being added to some Enum Material properties.
    ([UUM-134256](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134256))

- HDRP: Fixed a black Game view and Render Graph errors when using the ScreenSpaceAmbientOcclusion Fullscreen Debug Mode with MSAA.
    ([UUM-157335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157335))

- HDRP: Fixed a dark outline appearing around geometry against the sky when MSAA and a cloud layer are enabled.
    ([UUM-150911](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150911))

- HDRP: Fixed a water null exception when exiting the frame debugger.
    (UUM-141885)

- HDRP: Fixed an issue where FSR2 upscaler resources leaked GPU memory each time HDRP rebuilt the render pipeline, for example after a quality level change.
    (UUM-154186)

- HDRP: Fixed an issue where HDRP did not release the contexts and textures of upscaler framework upscalers when the render pipeline was rebuilt.
    (UUM-156238)

- HDRP: Fixed console errors, broken rendering, and a potential crash when entering Play Mode with Reload Domain disabled in the Enter Play Mode Options.
    ([UUM-147827](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147827))

- HDRP: Fixed incorrect shader stripping when "Lightmap Bicubic Sampling" was enabled.
    ([UUM-134794](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134794))

- HDRP: Fixed the Game view flickering when changing HDRP settings while the Game view is focused.
    ([UUM-147685](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147685))

- HDRP: Fixed water surfaces that disappeared for a moment in the Editor when a scene had water surfaces with different band counts.
    (UUM-141715)

- IL2CPP: Fix missing GC write barrier in `Thread.VolatileWrite(ref object, object)`.
    (UUM-155582)

- IL2CPP: Fix to attempt to skip intrinsifying intrinsic methods that are in the wrong assemblies, that can otherwise result in build errors.
    ([UUM-149211](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149211))

- IL2CPP: Fixed a C++ compile error when user code calls RuntimeHelpers.InitializeArray with a field handle that does not come directly from an ldtoken.
    ([UUM-154626](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154626))

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash when calling GetType\(\) through a boxed Nullable&lt;T&gt; that has no value, in generic code built with Faster \(smaller\) builds.
    (UUM-152751)

- IL2CPP: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- IL2CPP: Fixed an IL processing issue in generic code that caused a "No support for assigning to/from variable size types and concrete types" exception at build time.
    (UUM-147236)

- IL2CPP: Fixed case of FMA not being reported as available and case of AVX2 flag not being set in some cases.

- IL2CPP: Fixed crash on startup with the deep profiling support enabled.
    ([UUM-145576](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-profiler-application-crashes-immediately-after-launch-when-autoconnect-profiler-and-deep-profiling-support-are-enabled-in-build-settings))

- IL2CPP: Fixed GC marking on InlineArray types

- IL2CPP: Fixed issue were Type.IsAssignableFrom would throw a TypeInitializationException when called on a class whose static constructor had thrown.
    ([UUM-156129](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156129))

- IL2CPP: Fixed issue where memory from a thread object could be kept alive by the GC past its exit
    ([UUM-149541](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149541))

- IL2CPP: Fixed missing incremental GC write barriers on static and ThreadStatic fields on ref struct types.
    ([UUM-153622](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153622))

- IL2CPP: Fixed P/Invoke on Linux Players prepending the Player folder to an absolute `DllImport` library path, which hid the real path and load error in the resulting exception.
    ([UUM-155034](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155034))

- IL2CPP: Fixed segmentation fault on OSX and Linux while running IL2CPP.
    (UUM-149985)

- IL2CPP: Fixed typo checking VAR twice instead of MVAR.
    (UUM-130494)

- IL2CPP: Restored IL2CPP's code generation for ldind/stind opcodes to use normal pointer casts instead of memcpy calls.
    (UUM-129900)

- IMGUI: Fixed case of Advanced Dropdown menus, such as the Build Profiles "Add Settings" dropdown, showing unstyled red items when opened during Play Mode.
    ([UUM-135953](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135953))

- IMGUI: Fixed case of content in ImguiContainers not reacting to click when partially clipped.
    ([UUM-148486](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148486))

- IMGUI: Fixed IMGUI text drawing a few pixels past the borders of enclosing scroll views and containers when the text overflowed its control.
    ([UUM-150041](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150041))

- IMGUI: Fixed IMGUI text fields, such as Transform values in the Inspector, sometimes showing another value's text \(for example 0 and 1 swapped\) while a window was being resized.
    ([UUM-147879](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147879))

- IMGUI: Fixed property field child fields not being drawn when the property rect is at negative GUI coordinates.
    ([UUM-149255](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149255))

- IMGUI: Fixed text fields not scrolling to keep the cursor visible when typing text longer than the visible field width.
    ([UUM-149213](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149213))

- IMGUI: Fixed the text cursor being vertically misaligned in an empty text field.
    ([UUM-145875](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145875))

- IMGUI: Reduced GC allocations when a GUISkin style is assigned during OnGUI.
    ([UUM-134155](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134155))

- Input: Fixed a regression that added roughly 60 ms to editor project load time when input devices were first detected.
    (UUM-148220)

- Input: Removed a 17 B main-thread GC allocation that occurred on every input update tick in Mono players.
    (UUM-147633)

- Input System: Added `BiasCorrectedGyroscope`, reporting angular velocity in radians per second with the sensor bias removed, as the parity equivalent of `UnityEngine.Input.gyro.rotationRateUnbiased`. The device is present only on platforms that can supply a bias-corrected value, so check `BiasCorrectedGyroscope.current` for `null` before using it.

- Input System: Added `LocationSensor`, exposing GPS position, `status`, and permission state as the parity equivalent of `UnityEngine.Input.location`. Reports status via a new Input System `LocationServiceStatus` enum.

- Input System: Fixed a potential crash when an iOS application shut down with a motion sensor enabled. Pending sensor callbacks could run after the objects they wrote into had been destroyed.

- Input System: Fixed case of Android sensor devices going permanently silent after a device reset. Resetting a sensor released the underlying OS sensor while the device still reported itself as enabled, so no further values arrived and re-enabling it had no effect.

- Input System: Fixed issue on macOS where third party tools sending repeated key down events without a key up could result in keys becoming stuck.
    ([UUM-151878](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151878))

- Input System: Fixed keys held when a Windows Player lost focus staying pressed after the Player regained focus.
    ([UUM-157482](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157482))

- Input System: Fixed MakeUniqueName overflow and Device Simulator help text.

- Input System: Fixed misaligned labels in the Input Actions editor properties pane.

- Input System: Fixed polling for interaction phases \(such as `WasPerformedThisFrame`\) being missed during `Update` due to interaction timeouts sometimes occurring during the later before-render period when a before-render device \(such as an XR HMD\) is added. This means that timeouts and interactions like Hold may now wait to perform until the next frame instead of the earliest chance during before-render.
    ([UUM-147719](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147719))

- Input System: Fixed the error "type is not a supported string value" that was logged in the Console when viewing binding properties in the Input Actions Editor.
    ([UUM-155527](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155527))

- Input System: Fixed the iOS sensors backed by device motion so that `GravitySensor`, `AttitudeSensor` and `LinearAccelerationSensor` are each enabled and disabled independently. Enabling one of them previously started all of them, so code that enabled one device and read another now reads default values until it enables that device too.

- Input System: Fixed the Read more link in the Update Mode warning of the Input System settings opening a documentation page that does not describe Update Mode.
    ([UUM-154791](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154791))

- Input System: Fixed touch input from Unity Remote 5 being ignored when using the Input System package.
    ([UUM-155327](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155327))

- Input System: This fixes Windows' Xbox Game Bar and macOS overlays \(Spotlight et al\). On Windows, Steam/Discord/NVIDIA overlays work as always.
    ([UUM-150045](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150045))

- iOS: Bumped iOS/tvOS minspec from 15.0 to 16.0.

- iOS: Fixed a bug where pulling down the iOS Control Center could cause a Unity app to stop playing back audio.
    ([UUM-145522](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-audio-is-cut-out-when-accessing-ios-control-center))

- iOS: Fixed a crash when `application:openURL` or `application:continueUserActivity` was called before Unity initialized.
    ([UUM-151801](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151801))

- iOS: Fixed a false "Touch was already deallocated" error on iOS when the app moved to the background.
    ([UUM-145381](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-when-application-is-sent-to-background-with-active-touch-error-message-is-printed))

- iOS: Fixed App Store validation failing for projects whose minimum OS version is higher<br>
    than the one declared by the embedded UnityRuntime.framework.
    ([UUM-151138](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151138))

- iOS: Fixed building UnityFramework directly via command line.
    (UUM-151410)

- iOS: Fixed GameAssembly ignoring the target OS version from Player Settings.
    (UUM-113588)

- iOS: Fixed incomplete privacy manifest when integrating only UnityFramework.framework \(Unity as a Library\).

- iOS: Fixed occasional failure to merge render passes with msaa depth on apple gpu family 1-3.
    ([UUM-147831](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147831))

- iOS: Fixed ScrollView not responding to mouse wheel input with a connected mouse.
    ([UUM-141279](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141279))

- iOS: Fixed shader warmup on older devices \(AppleFamily3 and earlier\).
    ([UUM-147933](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147933))

- iOS: Fixed the secondary display mirroring the device screen instead of showing its own camera view on iOS 27.
    (UUM-149106)

- iOS: Fixed TouchScreenKeyboard.area returning an empty rect when the keyboard is open and the project uses the Objective-C trampoline.
    (UUM-147354)

- Kernel: Added a `JobHandle.ToString` implementation to prevent potential boxing.

- Kernel: Fixed a potential deadlock in the job system involving many external threads and manual jobs.
    (UUM-143073)

- Kernel: Fixed crash in DomainUnloadAutoFree memory tracker.
    ([UUM-144552](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144552))

- Kernel: Fixed crash in NativeSlice caused by 32bit integer overflow.
    (UUM-154594)

- Kernel: Fixed crash in UnsafeList::RemoveRange\(\) if count overflows a 32bit integer.
    (UUM-155334)

- Kernel: FixedString operator==\(String\) threw ArgumentException when the string exceeded the FixedString capacity.
    ([UUM-129279](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentexception-is-thrown-when-comparing-fixedstring64bytes-with-a-string-where-the-fixedstring64bytes-is-on-the-left-hand-side-of-equals-equals-1))

- Kernel: Windows standalone players crashed immediately on startup on CPUs without SSE4.2 support.
    ([UUM-155744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155744))

- License: Bump ProxyHelper version to allow running on case-sensitive filesystem.
    ([UUM-155895](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155895))

- License: Fixed an issue where the Editor continued without a license, and exited with code 0 in batch mode, when it could not connect to the Unity Licensing Client.
    ([UUM-151817](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151817))

- License: Fixed the license error dialog looping on Retry and close when two Editors of the same version required incompatible Licensing Client versions.
    (UUM-153121)

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when running without an initialized video subsystem, such as in batch mode with -nographics or without a display.
    ([UUM-156559](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156559))

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when the Editor was launched with -nographics but without -batchmode; the Editor now reports the invalid arguments and exits with code 1.
    ([UUM-154576](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154576))

- Linux: Fixed an issue where D-Pad inputs mapped to the wrong directions when using a wireless Xbox 360 controller on newer Kernel versions.
    ([UUM-136083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136083))

- Linux: Fixed bugs when building Windows Players and creating Visual Studio Solutions from Linux Editors.
    ([UUM-140187](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linuxeditor-building-for-windows-throws-agilitysdk-dll-could-not-be-retrieved-when-create-visual-studio-solution-was-enabled-before-switching-build-platform-to-windows))

- Linux: Fixed case where first touch input is dropped.
    (UUM-150086)

- Linux: Fixed Linux Player file extension stacking when building Linux Player from Mac Editor.
    (UUM-135833)

- Linux: Fixed some Xbox controller inputs are mapped to different buttons than they should be in Unity Input System when the controller is connected through Bluetooth on Linux.
    ([UUM-144216](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144216))

- Linux: Fixed the Editor hanging on the splash screen when a dialog opened while a scene was loading.
    ([UUM-151324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151324))

- Linux: Fixed the splash screen not fading out smoothly on start.
    ([UUM-140538](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ubuntu-player-splashscreen-does-not-fade-into-the-first-scene-unlike-shown-in-the-preview))

- Linux: Lowered the glibc version to 2.34.
    (UUM-150342)

- macOS: Allow the system to sleep when Screen.sleepTimeout is set to SleepTimeout.SystemSetting and the System Setting is not Never.
    ([UUM-150222](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150222))

- macOS: Fixed a crash on recent macOS versions that could occur when importing X.509 certificates.
    (UUM-154511)

- macOS: Fixed a top-level menu returning to the wrong position in the menu bar after being removed and restored.

- macOS: Fixed libMonoPosixHelper.dylib not being found in macOS server builds.
    ([UUM-141263](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-server-cannot-find-libmonoposixhelper-dot-dylib-when-calling-gzipstream))

- macOS: Fixed macOS Dedicated Server builds using CoreCLR failing to launch because the CoreCLR library could not be found.
    (UUM-155782)

- macOS: Fixed the Editor slowing down in batch mode and automated test runs on macOS when one of its windows lost focus.

- macOS: Fixed the macOS Editor ignoring internal tick requests while its window was unfocused, which made async and coroutine-based execution run slower than when focused.
    (UUM-151097)

- macOS: Fixed the splash screen not fading out smoothly on start.
    ([UUM-140536](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-player-splashscreen-does-not-fade-into-the-first-scene-unlike-shown-in-the-preview))

- macOS: Fixed the standalone player window title not using the localized application name.
    ([UUM-145924](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-app-window-title-is-not-localized-when-built-from-generated-xcode-project))

- Mono: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- Mono: Fixed race condition during interface initialization on ARM64.
    (UUM-144819)

- Mono: Fixed WebSocket connections to servers only supporting TLS 1.3.

- Mono: Stopped a crash when using a default GCHandle
    ([UUM-153835](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153835))

- Multiplayer: Console error logged when deleting the active Play Mode Scenario.
    ([UUM-144445](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144445))

- Multiplayer: Deleting an activated virtual player from the scenario configuration removed it from the config without stopping the running instance, leaving it orphaned.
    (UUM-138111)

- Multiplayer: Editor crash when the GPU Resident Drawer is enabled and a cloned object's rendering components were stripped by the Dedicated Server role.
    ([UUM-147692](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147692))

- Multiplayer: Editor window layouts loads a broken "Failed to load" Multiplayer Play Mode window and throws a NullReferenceException when docking around it in projects where Multiplayer Play Mode is not enabled or its package is not installed.
    ([UUM-146870](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146870))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a crash on macOS when opening the Edit menu while a Multiplayer Play Mode virtual player was focused.
    ([UUM-155708](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155708))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when the Launching Scenario window is opened without an active scenario, which left the window empty.
    ([UUM-151897](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151897))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a `NullReferenceException` that occurred when activating an Additional Editor Instance for the first time from a custom Play Mode Scenario.
    ([UUM-148037](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148037))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where error messages were thrown when selecting the Prefab submenu option on a GameObject in the clone players' Hierarchy window by removing the Prefab interactions on all Clone players.

- Multiplayer: Fixed errors thrown when using Terrain tools in Multiplayer Play Mode additional Editor instances Authoring tools are not supported and are now disabled.
    ([UUM-148239](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148239))

- Multiplayer: Fixed missing sample IDs for the Competitive and Turn-Based Multiplayer Center QuickStart genres, and made the sample README reliably selected and pinged after import.

- Multiplayer: Fixed scrolling so it now works correctly when the cursor hovers over the Complexity bar.

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Focus on Player option in the Multiplayer Play Mode window staying enabled and doing nothing when the player is not active.
    ([UUM-145368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145368))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the install button so it no longer displays "Installing package\(s\)..." after users cancel the install dialog.

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Multiplayer Play Mode window and virtual players not working after Multiplayer Play Mode was disabled and re-enabled in Preferences.
    (UUM-153152)

- Multiplayer: Fixed the scaling artifacts of the Multiplayer Center window's genre titles in Bitmap rendering mode.

- Multiplayer: Fixed virtual players crashing when the main editor is closed on Ubuntu.

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer PlayMode clone editors now adhere to the Hierarchy window version preference. When switching between Scene Hierarchy and the new Hierarchy window in the main Editor, it is now automatically reflected across all active clones.

- Multiplayer: On sample import, the scene that opens is now the initial scene named by the sample's play mode scenario instead of a fixed file name. Samples whose scenario names no scene, or names one outside the sample, keep the current scene open and log a warning.

- Multiplayer: Play Mode button remaining highlighted after canceling the Unsaved Changes prompt when entering Multiplayer Play Mode.
    ([UUM-149873](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149873))

- Multiplayer: Removed the Tree creation option from the hierarchy menu to prevent asset creation in Multiplayer Playmode Virtual Players, as asset authoring is forbidden.
    ([UUM-148223](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148223))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Correctly recreate UDP sockets on iOS when they are closed by the OS when an application is in the background for a while. Prevents a condition when the driver seemingly stops receiving packets.
    ([UUM-153993](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153993))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed a memory leak when closing connections using DTLS or TLS.
    ([UUM-151755](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151755))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed an issue where sending packets on UDP connections that don't have a remote peer anymore could eventually result in exhausting the receive queue, causing the server to not be able to receive anything anymore.
    (UUM-151844)

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed an issue with the `NetcodeInterop` layer which would cause connection failures when using NGO transports that use a single buffer for all receive operations \(for example the community Facepunch transport does this\).
    (UUM-151803)

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Network interfaces are now automatically disposed of before domain reloads. Without this protection, socket handles could be leaked which would cause errors when trying to re-bind to the same port later on. Note that this isn't a replacement for properly disposing of `NetworkDriver` objects; it's only a protection in cases where that's not done.
    ([UUM-146443](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146443))

- N/A \(internal\): Disabling known failures on UGK CI.

- N/A \(internal\): Fixing configuration deletion issue

- N/A \(internal\): Removing unneeded features in remote settings.

- Netcode for Entities: A ghosted FixedList - that is cleared and later repopulated with the same data \(e.g. during prediction rollback\) - now replicates correctly.
    ([UUM-131602](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131602))

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed a predicted ghost divergence caused by running at very high framerates with very low SimulationTickRate, which meant that the client to not even fully predict one tick ahead of the latest snapshot arrival, causing the snapshot to be applied on a partial tick, which in turn resulted in the prediction-history backup failing to write, so it would constantly be stale.
    (UUM-147343)

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed a regression in the handling of partial sends. In rare cases, the same dynamic ghost chunk hits the partial boundary at roughly the same entity index every tick. This indefinitely causes a subset of its ghosts to no longer be added to the snapshot.
    (UUM-145646)

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed a spurious "JobTempAlloc has allocations that are more than the maximum lifespan of 4 frames old" warning that appeared when baking a ghost prefab set to Owner Predicted mode without a GhostOwner component.

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed dragging the `Importance` field below its minimum of 1 \(or `Max Send Rate` below 0\) in the Ghost Authoring inspector triggering a re-bake pop-up even though the clamped value did not actually change.

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed issue with a ghost prefab having multiple ghost prefab entity instances, like when it's baked into multiple loaded subscenes. When loading, unloading and reloading the subscenes the ghost collection prefab would point to a previously unloaded/deleted entity prefab. This now properly gets updated to an instance created from the reloaded subscenes.
    ([UUM-146631](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146631))

- Netcode for Entities: GhostReceiveSystem's owner-switch trigger now enqueues an OwnerSwithchingEntry when GhostOwner.NetworkId changes, not just when it changes from zero.

- Netcode for Entities: Issue where predicted ghosts - that changed archetypes multiple times \(often moving back to their original archetype\) - would mispredict, particularly on partial ticks. Also fixed this issue with enable bits and child components.
    ([UUM-131597](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131597))

- Netcode for Entities: Styling of several elements of GhostAuthoringInspectionComponent no longer conflicts with light theme default editor styling. This caused some elements to be barely visible or entirely unreadable \(UUM-148863\).
    ([UUM-148863](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148863))

- Networking: Bumps libcurl library to 8.20.

- Networking: De-referencing invalid texture pointer when a webrequest is teared down abruptly.
    ([UUM-144540](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144540))

- Networking: Fixed a possible editor-only crash when using a custom certificate handler with UnityWebRequest in long editor sessions involving many domain reloads.
    ([UUM-147347](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147347))

- Networking: Fixed a situation where Curl errors coming from UnityWebRequest could fail to be logged appropriately, making issues harder to debug. Note that even in this situation the failure would still be reported correctly to calling code. This bug only impacted logging.
    ([UUM-145433](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unitywebrequest-can-lose-error-messages-from-curl))

- Networking: Fixed an issue in UnityWebRequest where an error 26 or 65 \("necessary data rewind was not possible"\) would sometimes be logged when resuming upload requests.
    ([UUM-144767](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webrequest-missing-curlopt-seekfunction-causes-curl-error-26-on-post-retry-with-reused-connections))

- Networking: Fixed issue where `UnityHttpMessageHandler` requests would never complete \(and could not be cancelled\) when the HTTP response had an empty body. \(UUM-150583\).
    ([UUM-150583](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150583))

- Networking: Fixed the search and summary rows in the Webrequest Profiler shrinking as the request list filled.

- Networking: Fixed UnityWebRequest cookies occasionally reverting to an older value when a slower request completed after a faster concurrent request to the same host had already set a newer cookie.
    ([UUM-150958](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150958))

- Networking: Reject TLS connections to servers using the compromised eMudhra certificates \(CVE-2026-111943\).
    (UUM-158231)

- Networking: Unity Transport: fix a possible failure to re-establish a connection when re-homing \(when the local IP address changes\) if using DTLS.
    (UUM-154831)

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix a small memory leak that would occur when creating TLS/DTLS sessions.
    ([UUM-151755](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151755))

- Networking: Unity Transport: When a DTLS connection fails because of a wrong certificate, the reported disconnection reason is now the more correct `AuthenticationFailure` instead of `MaxConnectionAttempts`.
    (UUM-159110)

- Networking: UnityWebRequest: Don't URL-encode characters '&amp;' and '=' in `PostWwwForm`.
    (UUM-149976)

- Networking: UnityWebRequest: Fixed a freeze when quitting a standalone player while an ongoing request was still resolving DNS against an unresponsive or slow DNS server.
    ([UUM-151643](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151643))

- Package: Collections: Fixed FixedString formatting of certain float values when CoreCLR is the scripting backend.

- Package Manager: Added warning log messages when importing Untrusted or Limited Trust Asset Packages in Batch Mode or CLI.

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where a newly created package was not using the organization the project belongs to in the technical name.
    ([UUM-148110](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148110))

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where samples weren't discoverable for locally installed UPM packages that were also available on the Asset Store.
    (UUM-147778)

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where switching pages quickly after clearing the search bar results in an empty package list.
    ([UUM-134027](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/my-assets-in-package-manager-appear-empty-when-the-search-is-cleared-and-the-tab-is-switched))

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where the import status did not display for imported samples in the Sample tab.
    (UUM-146766)

- Package Manager: Fixed exporting a signed .unitypackage failing with "IOException: Stream was too long." when the uncompressed package content exceeded 2 GB, and reduced Editor memory usage during package export and signing.
    ([UUM-153848](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153848))

- Package Manager: Fixed importing a .unitypackage through CLI shows limited trust, drag-and-dropping the same package shows full trust.
    (UUM-148788)

- Package Manager: Fixed issue where install progress spinner for a selected package disappears when another package is selected.
    (UUM-139184)

- Package Manager: Fixed package install operations hanging when the project contains packages with entitlement errors.
    (UUM-141909)

- Package Manager: Fixed so the package search bar is cleared if the package being opened through the API isn't visible.
    ([UUM-132102](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132102))

- Package Manager: Fixed stale string when searching and there are no results found.
    ([UUM-143121](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-results-for-x-text-in-the-package-manager-search-is-not-updated-when-edited-search-string-has-no-matches-as-well))

- Package Manager: Fixed the Editor becoming unresponsive when the package trust window appeared during Editor initialization.
    ([UUM-153544](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153544))

- Package Manager: Fixed the issue where some packages have incorrect signature icon on Unity start up time.
    ([UUM-143201](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/signed-registry-packages-show-green-shield-icon-when-show-pre-release-package-versions-is-toggled-on))

- Package Manager: Fixed the refresh page to also refresh user entitlement licences.

- Package Manager: Importing a .unityPackage inside a .unityPackage coming from the Asset Store will not get a warning for being unsigned since it is considered coming from a trusted source.
    (UUM-154234)

- Package Manager: Improved the error message displayed when adding an incompatible version with the current Unity Editor.
    (UUM-138842)

- Package Manager: New event triggered on sample import, before Asset Database refresh.
    (UUM-142221)

- Package Manager: Package Manager related errors are thrown for Play Mode Scenarios additional editors when activated.
    ([UUM-147009](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147009))

- Package Manager: Passive trust pop-ups are not cutoff anymore.
    ([UUM-131817](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131817))

- Package Manager: Removed the recently introduced `upmSign` batch-mode command. It requires more thought and validation on the product side.
    (UUM-146198)

- Package Manager: Sample parent package name for UPM on Asset Store is using the package name not the product name.
    (UUM-145405)

- Particles: Fixed "'SafeNormalize': implicit truncation of vector type" warning coming from Particles shaders.
    ([UUM-147453](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147453))

- Particles: Fixed Camera Fade OFF + Soft Particles ON combination making particles disappear on some devices.
    ([UUM-146249](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146249))

- Particles: Shape Asset references should now reset properly.
    ([UUM-141007](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141007))

- Performance Test Framework: Fixed error where RunSettings would not be properly reset when Domain Reload didn't happen at entering playmode.
    (UUM-158376)

- Performance Test Framework: Repeat test-player builds no longer assign a fresh GUID to the PerformanceTestRunInfo.json/PerformanceTestRunSettings.json assets on every build. Previously, deleting these assets' `.meta` files as part of post-build cleanup caused Unity to assign a new GUID on the next build, which the data pipeline treated as a change and triggered a full asset rebuild even when nothing else changed. The `.meta` files are now cached in `Library/` between builds and restored before the assets are rewritten, scoped to builds with test assemblies.

- Performance Test Framework: SampleGroups are now getting cleared of samples and statistics a test used, after its result has been reported. This stops data bleeding in other tests in case tests hold on to SampleGroups for some reason.

- Physics: An "Incremental broadphase integrity check failed" exception thrown every step after enabling `PhysicsStep.IncrementalStaticBroadphase` in a world that was first simulated with no rigid bodies, and missing collisions and incremental broadphase integrity check failures after toggling `PhysicsStep.IncrementalStaticBroadphase` or `IncrementalDynamicBroadphase` off and on again at runtime.
    (UUM-155824)

- Physics: ComputePenetration between a scaled Sphere and Convex Mesh Collider will now return the correct result.
    (UUM-139615)

- Physics: Fixed a crash with ArticulationBody component hierarchies due to the 64 link depth limit not being correctly enforced.
    ([UUM-145530](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145530))

- Physics: Fixed an issue inside the PhysX 4 sdk, where CharacterController wouldn't be able to collide with more than 100 shapes at a time.
    ([UUM-132204](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132204))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where a mesh collider on a static body, would not have it's sub-aabbs recalculated when using incremental static broadphase. This lead to the collider being ignored by queries such as raycasts.
    ([UUM-138528](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138528))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where baking a uniform scaled a static root, would not handle scales different than 1.0f correctly.
    (UUM-133010)

- Physics: Fixed an issue where ConfigurableJoint setups would jitter once they reached their distance constraint maximum, due to their contact distance not being handled correctly.
    ([UUM-146852](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146852))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where Joint components wouldn't cause newly connected Rigidbody components to re-evaluate their contacts. So flags such as "enable collisions" could take effect.
    ([UUM-156450](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156450))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where replacing a PhysicsCollider's BlobAsset reference in place would not get picked up when using the incremental static broadphase feature. Thus causing queries such as raycasts to report incorrect data.
    ([UUM-138528](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138528))

- Physics: Fixed an issue with Rigidbody component serialization where prefab overrides \(for drag/angular drag\) saved in scenes on 2022LTS wouldn't be retained after upgrading.
    ([UUM-147601](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147601))

- Physics: Fixed compilation warnings and runtime errors in `ModifyJointLimitsAuthoring`.
    ([UUM-145822](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145822))

- Physics: Fixed Rigidbody.mass accepting a NaN value, which made the body pass through all colliders.
    ([UUM-154143](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154143))

- Physics: Improved the cleanup performance of IgnoreCollision when destroying a Collider.
    ([UUM-115186](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-115186))

- Physics: Prevented manual edits to the `SimulationSingleton` value in `Unity.Physics`. Change the physics simulation type from the `PhysicsSimulationStep` component instead.
    ([UUM-148731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148731))

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsWorld.DrawQueryResult" now uses "drawNormal" to decide whether to draw normals, instead of "drawPoint".

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsWorld.drawTarget", "PhysicsBody.drawTarget", "PhysicsShape.drawTarget" and "PhysicsJoint.drawTarget" now log a warning and keep their current value when assigned a value that is not a valid "PhysicsWorld.DrawTarget".
    (UUM-154002)

- Physics 2D: Added "PhysicsBody.totalForce" and "PhysicsBody.totalTorque" getters and setters.
    ([UUM-144878](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/physicsbody-cannot-set-slash-get-the-total-torce-or-torque))

- Physics 2D: Added "PhysicsWorld.bounds" property to get the current bounds of all PhysicsShape in the PhysicsWorld.
    (UUM-145746)

- Physics 2D: Added "PhysicsWorld.drawOrder" and "PhysicsWorldDefinition.drawOrder" used to sort the order in which the physics renderer draws world. This provides direct control so you can better visualize multiple worlds; especially important when rendering non-transparent content. In addition, "PhysicsWorld.drawTarget" and "PhysicsWorldDefinition.drawTarget" was added to control if Game view and/or Scene view are rendered to.
    (UUM-145746)

- Physics 2D: Added a small left and right panel padding to the Project Settings page for PhysicsCore2D module so it doesn't touch the window border.

- Physics 2D: Added drawing of collision normal \(opposite one-way side\) for ChainSegmentGeometry.
    (UUM-145454)

- Physics 2D: Added missing definition get/set property from all joints to match all other physics objects.
    (UUM-145454)

- Physics 2D: Added missing physicscore2d Transform / InverseTransform for geometry spans of Circle, Capsule, Polygon, Segment and ChainSegment.
    (UUM-145454)

- Physics 2D: Added missing public argument validation for various Physics Core 2D methods.
    ([UUM-156367](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156367))

- Physics 2D: Added PhysicsWorld cloning, snapshot and restore. "PhysicsWorld.Clone\(\)" to clone a world, "PhysicsWorld.CreateSnapshot\(\)" to create a complete snapshot of the content of the world and "PhysicsWorld.ApplySnapshot\(\)" to apply a previous snapshot to the PhysicsWorld. Finally, the ability to create a new world with a previous snapshot with "PhysicsWorld.Create\(snapshot\)" overload.
    (UUM-145746)

- Physics 2D: Added the ability to disable the auto-clearing of custom drawing lifetimes.  When a draw command expires, it is typically removed but setting "PhysicsWorld.autoClearCustom" to false will mean the drawing persists until you explicitly call "PhysicsWorld.ClearDraw\(\)".
    (UUM-145746)

- Physics 2D: Applying a very large wind force via `PhysicsBody.ApplyWind`, or a very large impulse via `PhysicsWorld.Explode`, could silently corrupt the entire physics world with NaN or Infinity values.
    ([UUM-150029](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150029))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "CapsuleGeometry.Intersect", several other geometry "Intersect" methods and "PhysicsQuery.ShapeAndShape" returning the contact manifold for the two geometries in reverse order, with the normal pointing from the second geometry to the first.
    ([UUM-157419](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157419))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsAABB.CastRay" returning an incorrect fraction when "maxFraction" is below 1, and "PhysicsAABB.center" and "PhysicsAABB.extents" warnings naming the wrong member.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsBody.BatchImpulse.ApplyAngularImpulse" waking a sleeping body when "wake" is false.
    ([UUM-157591](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157591))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsHandle.AsWorld" so that it throws an exception for an invalid world instead of returning a handle reported as a pool handle.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsLayers" so that "GetLayerMask" includes every layer sharing a name, "GetLayerName" returns an empty string for an unnamed layer, and null or out-of-range arguments throw exceptions that name the correct parameter.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsLayers.GetLayerMask" and "PhysicsLayers.GetLayerOrdinal" returning layer 3 for an empty layer name when physics layers are off.
    ([UUM-157609](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157609))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMask" so that a "LayerMask" including layer 31 no longer sets the upper 32 bits, "AreBitsSet" returns false for an empty mask, a default "ResetBitIterator" includes bit 0, and shift counts outside 0 to 63 throw an exception.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix" returning an incorrect translation on the XZ and ZY transform planes.
    ([UUM-157407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157407))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D" placing geometry away from where the target Transform is drawn when the source Transform is turned a half turn about an axis in the 2D plane, the target is under a non-uniform scale, or the target is tilted out of the plane.
    ([UUM-157407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157407))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate" so that converting to a "Quaternion" no longer doubles the angle, "isNormalized" no longer logs a warning for a rotation that is not normalized, "ToString" labels the angle as degrees, and "Normalized" warnings name the correct method.
    ([UUM-157163](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157163))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate.Rotate" rotating by less than the requested angle, by noticeably less for large angles.
    ([UUM-157606](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157606))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate.RotateVector", "PhysicsRotate.InverseRotateVector", "PhysicsTransform.TransformPoint", "PhysicsTransform.InverseTransformPoint", "PhysicsAABB.center" and "PhysicsAABB.extents" returning a different value on each call for an invalid argument instead of zero.
    ([UUM-157608](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157608))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsTransform" validation warnings naming the wrong method.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "Rigidbody2D.Slide" from reporting an invalid "startPosition" argument incorrectly displaying the "gravity" argument.
    ([UUM-154949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154949))

- Physics 2D: Fixed a bug where a dynamic body with a rotation constraint would bounce off surfaces despite zero bounciness.
    ([UUM-150990](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150990))

- Physics 2D: Fixed a crash caused by unbounded recursion when a `PhysicsEvents.WorldDefinitionChange` handler wrote the same world's definition. Changed the nested write to apply without raising a second change event.

- Physics 2D: Fixed a warning about an invalid NaN angle when a Physics Pose is rotated exactly a half turn about an axis in the 2D plane.

- Physics 2D: Fixed an issue where identically named words expanding the PhysicsCore2D profiler both become expanded. Now any previously expanded world is kept.
    ([UUM-153434](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153434))

- Physics 2D: Fixed case of the time of impact query returning incorrect values for its "point", "normal" and "impactState". Also fixed the segment distance query, whose "distance" property was returning the squared distance. That property now returns the true distance, and a new "sqrDistance" property returns the squared distance for when you want to avoid the cost of a square root.

- Physics 2D: Fixed case of `PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D` and `PhysicsMath.GetRelativePose2D` returning a non-finite rotation for a transform with a negative scale on one axis, and `PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D` applying the scale of the axis that its transform plane ignores.

- Physics 2D: Fixed errors reported when a Transform registered with "PhysicsWorld.RegisterTransformChange" was destroyed after a Play Mode transition. Calling "PhysicsWorld.UnregisterTransformChange" after such a transition did not fully unregister the Transform, so an error was reported when it was later destroyed. This happened with any Transform that outlives a Play Mode transition, such as one in a Prefab Mode preview scene.

- Physics 2D: Fixed extrapolated bodies rotating backwards between simulation steps on the XZ and ZY transform planes.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PhysicsMask", "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" and "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" inspectors setting the upper 32 bits of the mask when layer 31 or Everything is selected with physics layers off.
    ([UUM-157604](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157604))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "ShowAsPhysicsMask" attribute being ignored on a "PhysicsMask", "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" or "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" field nested in a struct or held in an array or List.
    ([UUM-157616](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157616))

- Physics 2D: Reading `PhysicsWorld.counters`, or other queries, after restoring a world from a corrupted or truncated `Snapshot` could crash the Editor or Player.
    ([UUM-150072](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150072))

- Physics 2D: Removed the reservation limit for how many PhysicsWorld can be created. Now memory is only allocated for the PhysicsWorld that are created with a maximum limit of 65535.  You can set the "PhysicsCoreSettings2D.initialWorldCapacity" to reserve the capacity but it will grow accordingly.
    (UUM-145746)

- Physics 2D: Removing double-spaces from Collider2D help-box text.
    ([UUM-147075](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147075))

- Physics 2D: Wake Rigidbody2D when activating a child GameObject containing a Collider2D.
    ([UUM-149852](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149852))

- Physics 2D: `PhysicsWorld` could crash or corrupt memory when created with a very large or negative `PhysicsWorldDefinition.capacity`.
    ([UUM-150067](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150067))

- Player: Added guard to avoid hitting a debug assert in dev player builds.
    (UUM-152365)

- Player: Fixed an issue with accidental shell invocations with Bee and rc.exe.
    (UUM-108834)

- Player: GameObjects cast correct shadows after Shader.globalMaximumLOD is changed at runtime.
    ([UUM-150226](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150226))

- Prefabs: Fixed a non-determinism issue when importing a prefab in-process versus out-of-process with parallel import.

- Prefabs: Fixed case of Prefab property overrides recorded under a field's former serialized name \(via FormerlySerializedAs\) not being recognized or revertible on the renamed property.
    ([UUM-151415](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151415))

- Prefabs: Prefab Overrides comparison popup's Apply button no longer overlaps the popup's outline.
    ([UUM-132319](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132319))

- Profiler: Added CoreCLR capture of GC-static field values and frozen/non-GC heap chunks to the memory snapshot format. Bumped format to v20.

- Profiler: Fix to hide the "Screenshot details are paused while the Profiler is recording" note as soon as profiling ends.
    ([UUM-148847](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148847))

- Profiler: Fixed a crash in `ProfilerSymbolsDelayedDeletion::AddCloneObject`.
    ([UUM-144194](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-profilersymbolsdelayeddeletion-addcloneobject-when-deserializing-an-object-instantiate-marker-with-a-dangling-slash-corrupt-name-string))

- Profiler: Fixed alignment on date label in Profiler captures list.
    ([UUM-132276](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-captures-list-date-title-is-not-centered-vertically))

- Profiler: Fixed an inconsistent seam line between the image and frame number on Profiler Screenshots module thumbnails.
    ([UUM-152561](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152561))

- Profiler: Fixed an InvalidOperationException that could be thrown in the Profiler window when the Physics \(2D\) module was selected and another Editor window was maximized or restored.
    ([UUM-151943](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151943))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where directly loading profiling data on macOS did not filter to relevant file types \(.data, .raw\).
    ([UUM-139554](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-data-stream-has-invalid-signature-errors-are-thrown-when-loading-a-highlights-file-in-the-profiler))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where opening the profiler window with the physics CPU module open would throw a InvalidOperationException.
    ([UUM-143704](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/invalidoperationexception-is-thrown-when-reopening-profiler-window-with-physics-module-selected))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where resetting Profiler modules would leave \(duplicated\) custom modules until the window was re-opened.
    (UUM-148468)

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where the New Timeline view's Zoom when changing event setting had no effect.

- Profiler: Fixed black bars and missing section titles in the Memory module's details view.
    ([UUM-145754](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/bars-in-profilers-memory-module-are-colored-black))

- Profiler: Fixed case of a `NullReferenceException` being thrown when saving an empty Standalone Profiler recording.
    ([UUM-150588](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150588))

- Profiler: Fixed case of a `NullReferenceException` being thrown when saving Profiler data from the top menu of the Standalone Profiler window after toggling the UI Toolkit Live Reload option.
    ([UUM-150706](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150706))

- Profiler: Fixed case of screenshot info text remaining after profiling data was cleared.
    (UUM-149205)

- Profiler: Fixed CPU and GPU spikes in Play mode while the Profiler window is open.
    ([UUM-153018](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153018))

- Profiler: Fixed deadlock in PreloadManager  when taking Memory Profiler snapshot during load operation in progress.
    (UUM-143493)

- Profiler: Fixed frame-number labels overlapping in the Screenshots module's timeline view when Game View captures used a narrow aspect ratio.
    ([UUM-152532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152532))

- Profiler: Fixed Highlights module not redrawing captured frame data when re-enabled.
    ([UUM-144840](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/enabling-highlights-profiler-module-while-not-in-play-mode-does-not-redraw-already-captured-frame-data-until-the-view-is-refreshed))

- Profiler: Fixed inability to create a details view for CPU Usage module following deletion of a custom module.
    (UUM-149160)

- Profiler: Fixed incorrect screenshot counter in Profiler.
    (UUM-148466)

- Profiler: Fixed issues with renaming Profiler captures in specific situations on high-DPI desktop setups.
    ([UUM-143329](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143329))

- Profiler: Fixed labelling of Unrooted Allocations associated with GraphicsStateCollection Allocations.
    ([UUM-139555](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139555))

- Profiler: Fixed ObjectDisposedException errors thrown when maximizing the Profiler window with the CPU Usage New Timeline view selected.

- Profiler: Fixed Profiler details view potentially showing info for frames that are no longer present in the timeline.
    ([UUM-150694](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150694))

- Profiler: Fixed Project Auditor resetting the Group By, Show Ignored Issues, and Hierarchy view settings after a new analysis.
    (UUM-156587)

- Profiler: Fixed the CPU Usage module's New Timeline view not being restored when reopening the Profiler window.

- Profiler: Fixed the Highlights module's 'Target FPS' label being clipped when showing large values.
    ([UUM-137768](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/target-frame-time-text-in-the-profiler-window-does-not-fit-and-is-cut-off-when-target-frame-time-is-set-to-1000-fps))

- Profiler: Fixed the Jobs Info panel elements overlapping each other when the Captures list is resized wider in the CPU Usage New Timeline view.

- Profiler: Fixed the Jobs Info table flickering while inspecting live frame data in the CPU Usage New Timeline view.

- Profiler: Fixed the Profiler capture rename field discarding the first click and re-selecting the whole name instead of placing the caret.
    ([UUM-153075](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153075))

- Profiler: Fixed the Profiler capture Target Frame Time field removing the decimal separator, so entering a fractional frame rate such as 60.5 applied 605 instead.
    ([UUM-153093](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153093))

- Profiler: Fixed the Project Auditor's Open in Search button not carrying the search field query over into the Search window.
    ([UUM-147241](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147241))

- Profiler: Fixed the Rendering module chart mode resetting to Rendering when the Profiler window was closed and reopened.
    ([UUM-153203](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153203))

- Profiler: Fixed the Troubleshoot Connection Issues button in the Profiler linking to a documentation page unrelated to connection troubleshooting.
    ([UUM-153354](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153354))

- Profiler: Fixed the warning icon in the CPU Hierarchy column overlapping the sort indicator.
    ([UUM-137749](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-sorting-icon-in-the-warnings-sorting-section-has-little-to-no-space-between-the-sorting-triangle-icon-and-the-warning-icon-and-the-warning-icon-is-not-centered))

- Profiler: Memory Profiler under-reports ALLOC_TEMP_JOB_ASYNC footprint, causing unused blocks to appear as Untracked.
    ([UUM-145712](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145712))

- Profiler: Prevented pasting into the CPU Profiler's Sample Stack window.
    ([UUM-116908](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-sample-stack-window-allows-pasting-causing-window-ui-break))

- Profiler: Reimplemented an aggregate draw calls counter for profiling.

- Profiler: The Profiler's Capture List foldouts now open and close when clicking the title as well as the arrow.
    ([UUM-132277](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-captures-list-foldouts-do-not-open-when-clicked-on-the-foldout-title))

- Profiler: Updated the CPU Hierarchy column to use the standard warning icon.
    ([UUM-117083](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inconsistent-warning-icon-in-the-profilers-hierarchy-view))

- Profiler: Updated the CPU Hierarchy column to use the standard warning icon.
    ([UUM-131177](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warning-in-the-profiler-window-is-white-and-hard-to-see))

- Project Browser: Fixed an issue where pasting copied sub-assets into a folder overwrote existing assets with the same name and broke references to them.
    ([UUM-121005](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121005))

- QNX: Fixed case of Burst AOT compilation failing to link the generated library.
    (UUM-151399)

- QNX: Fixed case where first touch input is dropped.
    (UUM-150086)

- QNX: Fixed the player window not resizing when the resolution or fullscreen mode changed, which left the rendered content stretched or misplaced.
    (UUM-150071)

- QNX: Startup timing potential overflowing reported User time.
    (UUM-148079)

- Scene Manager: `SceneManager.GetSceneByLoadableSceneId` no longer returns a valid `Scene` for a default/invalid `LoadableSceneId` \(UUM-150020\).
    ([UUM-150020](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150020))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where an activated RectTool would start throwing errors if SceneView camera zoomed out far away from the selected object.
    ([UUM-146170](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146170))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where moving a GameObject far from the origin with the Move tool made its handle disappear and logged an invalid localPosition \(NaN\) error.
    ([UUM-146738](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146738))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed case of the Orientation Gizmo's negative axis arrows, direction labels, and rotation-lock icon changing color when the Center Axis color preference was changed.
    ([UUM-150607](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150607))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed issue where the Rect Tool would not be moveable if the caps lock modifier was on.
    ([UUM-150693](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150693))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed the SRP Volume gizmo not showing in the SceneView.
    ([UUM-146740](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146740))

- Scene/Game View: Improving grids rendering and performance in the SceneView.
    ([UUM-143208](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143208))

- Scripting: AutoStaticsCleanup should accept any declared type initializers.
    ([UUM-142742](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/autostaticscleanup-silently-does-nothing-when-applied-to-readonly-fields))

- Scripting: Fixed .NET 1.x dll's not getting loaded into the domain.
    ([UUM-141092](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141092))

- Scripting: Fixed a "Mismatched state in DefaultScenario" error when exiting Play Mode after editing a script with "Script Changes While Playing" set to "Recompile After Finished Playing".
    ([UUM-148454](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148454))

- Scripting: Fixed a console performance regression introduced by the virtual-memory EntityId allocator. EntityId \(instance ID\) allocation and release are significantly faster, especially under multithreaded create/destroy churn, and committed memory no longer grows under sustained object churn.
    (UUM-148756)

- Scripting: Fixed a false OperationCanceledException thrown when awaiting Task.Delay\(\) inside a background-thread Awaitable method.
    ([UUM-150034](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150034))

- Scripting: Fixed aliases conflicting when multiple namespaces with the same name use different alias sources, and the AutoStaticsCleanup is used.
    ([UUM-158160](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158160))

- Scripting: Fixed an Editor crash with the error "Size overflow in allocator" when JsonUtility parsed a string longer than 536,870,911 characters.
    ([UUM-156359](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156359))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where cancelling an Awaitable.NextFrameAsync or Awaitable.EndOfFrameAsync awaitable during a frame update could make an unrelated Awaitable complete early with a default result.
    ([UUM-156357](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156357))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where MonoBehaviours loaded from AssetBundles showed as missing scripts in Players when their assembly was loaded at runtime.
    ([UUM-159333](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159333))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where opening the Standalone Profiler could trigger a script compilation in the Profiler process, which logged "Could not find a part of the path" errors for package assemblies.
    (UUM-133483)

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where `AutoStaticsCleanup` didn't restore a static field that was set to `null` during a Play session.
    ([UUM-149874](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149874))

- Scripting: Fixed AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory returning the path of the application executable instead of the folder that contains it.
    (UUM-155647)

- Scripting: Fixed assembly resolutions errors in IL post processors that referenced assemblies with names starting with "Mono.Cecil".
    ([UUM-147560](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147560))

- Scripting: Fixed case of the `VBCSCompiler` background process staying alive after the Editor crashed or was force quit.
    (UUM-148978)

- Scripting: Fixed CompilationPipeline.ParseResponseFile not recognizing -unsafe- in a csc.rsp file, which caused ResponseFileData.Unsafe to ignore an explicit request to disable unsafe code.
    (UUM-154574)

- Scripting: Fixed exceptions not being logged when thrown from async Awaitable message methods such as Awake, Start, and OnEnable.
    ([UUM-155273](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155273))

- Scripting: Fixed FileNotFoundExceptions are constantly thrown if Unity Version Control package is installed.
    (UUM-148874)

- Scripting: Fixed InitializeOnLoad ordering logic to be more performant.
    (UUM-149218)

- Scripting: Fixed missing EditMode scope during startup and inconsistent timing of EditMode and PlayMode scope.
    (UUM-141834)

- Scripting: Fixed Plane.Equals returning true for planes with different hash codes, which caused HashSet and Dictionary lookups to fail.
    ([UUM-157345](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157345))

- Scripting: Fixed potential editor freezing for long periods when running ScriptUpdater.
    (UUM-154695)

- Scripting: Fixed the VBCSCompiler background process staying alive after the Editor was closed.<br>
    On Linux, the compiler pre-warm step also wrote its temporary files next to the temp directory instead of inside it; both are fixed by routing the pre-warm and shutdown through the bundled .NET SDK.
    (UUM-148978)

- Scripting: Fixed ~2 KB size increase in empty WebGL Player builds and removed `editor/coreclr` only functions.
    (UUM-147728)

- Scripting: Optimize GetSelectionCount object validation in SceneInspector.
    (UUM-155038)

- Search: ArgumentException thrown when typing `!` as the first character in a Search window query \(UUM-141246\).
    ([UUM-141246](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141246))

- Search: Fixed an Editor freeze that could occur when a search indexing task was canceled while the index was being updated.

- Search: Fixed an ObjectDisposedException when a search expression ended a query that was still running.
    (UUM-144924)

- Search: Fixed bug where the Add Scene option was present in the Search window's inspector for scenes located in read-only packages.
    ([UUM-139396](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139396))

- Search: Fixed funky apostrophe query when using "Find References in Scene".
    ([UUM-150994](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150994))

- Search: Fixed issue where the "Find References in Scene" component menu action would not work correctly in new Hierarchy.
    ([UUM-122332](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-122332))

- Search: Fixed issues where Asset Store search result items would have empty text when scrolling in List mode.
    ([UUM-140235](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140235))

- Search: Fixed search expressions such as count, empty and tonumber returning wrong results or errors while results were still loading.
    (UUM-150890)
