- Search: Fixed Search's ObjectField multi-value display.
    ([UUM-147741](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147741))

- Serialization: Dictionary Drawer now has a one-column display mode for narrow Inspectors.
    (UUM-146024)

- Serialization: Fixed a crash on project load when a \[SerializeReference\] field referenced a type that had been changed from a class to a struct.
    ([UUM-150957](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150957))

- Serialization: Fixed an issue in Dictionary serialization so it preserves entries with null keys in the same way it already preserved duplicate-key entries. The null-key rows in the UI are now marked with a warning icon as well so they can be identified and fixed \(set to a valid object reference\).
    (UUM-146664)

- Serialization: Fixed an issue where changing a serialized Object reference field to a list of that type created a list of "None" elements instead of an empty list.
    ([UUM-158335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158335))

- Serialization: Fixed Analyzer UAC1016 error flagging a Dictionary with generic types as cannot be serialized incorrectly
    (UUM-155167)

- Serialization: Fixed crash where WeakReferences were being destroyed off of the main thread, which contended with Mon's GC assumptions about WeakReferences only being freed from the main thread. This fixes an eventual crash inside the Garbage Collector.
    (UUM-143324)

- Serialization: Fixed the "+" button in the Inspector not adding more than one element to bool arrays and lists.
    ([UUM-159259](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159259))

- Serialization: Fixed \[SerializeReference\] objects referenced by a missing type skipping OnBeforeSerialize when their host is re-saved, which silently discarded state the callback persists. Regression introduced in 6000.6.0b1; never present in a full release.
    (UUM-149620)

- Serialization: SerializeReference fields being given the wrong reference, or none at all, when a serialized array or list of a class type contained a null element.
    (UUM-152214)

- Services: Fixed long cloud project names not being truncated in the Cloud project dropdown in Services settings.
    ([UUM-150192](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150192))

- Shadergraph: Added issue where custom render target preview on linux did not render correctly in some cases.
    ([UUM-143562](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-rendering-artifacts-appear-in-shader-graph-main-preview-after-removing-universal-active-target-and-adding-custom-render-texture-target))

- Shadergraph: Added issue where gradient picker would not respond to undo/redo commands.
    ([UUM-145371](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/redo-does-nothing-in-the-hdr-gradient-editor-when-using-the-redo-shortcut-in-the-shader-graph-window))

- Shadergraph: Added issue where gradient picker's color picker would misbehave during undo/redo.
    ([UUM-145356](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdr-gradient-editor-window-color-picker-closes-when-undoing-color-selection))

- Shadergraph: Added issue where NRE could result from renaming a subgraph dropdown.
    ([UUM-148866](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148866))

- Shadergraph: Added issue where particle shader sample would complain about missing cullMode property.
    ([UUM-146914](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146914))

- Shadergraph: Custom attributes on properties now copy-paste correctly.
    (UUM-142264)

- Shadergraph: Disabled unused keyboard navigation for blackboard foldouts.
    ([UUM-144951](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shadergraph-category-folder-doesnt-expand-slash-collapse-when-using-arrow-keys-to-navigate))

- Shadergraph: Ensure that the preview window for nodes can always be toggled.
    ([UUM-143463](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/expression-node-preview-window-cannot-be-collapsed-after-undoing-entered-expression-or-function))

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where float enum entry names were changing when other edits were taking place.
    ([UUM-143243](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/entries-are-renamed-and-only-single-input-is-accepted-when-changing-float-enum-entry-values))

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where Template ordering would not work properly in the Shader Graph Template Window.
    ([UUM-143067](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143067))

- Shadergraph: Fixed heatmap undo/redo for subgraph entries.
    ([UUM-142683](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/asset-gets-unselected-and-added-subgraphs-list-item-is-not-undone-when-performing-undo-in-heatmap-with-default-values-asset-inspector))

- Shadergraph: Fixed invalid characters in generated shaders under the Turkish regional format.
    ([UUM-142088](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shader-graph-generates-i-in-the-shader-when-using-turkish-regional-format))

- Shadergraph: Fixed issue where errors would trigger when particle shader sample was imported.
    ([UUM-146912](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146912))

- Shadergraph: Fixed the "implicit truncation of vector type" compiler warning in the Shader Graph Custom Lighting samples.
    ([UUM-155156](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155156))

- Shadergraph: Handle error message when deleting a group with a subgraph output node.
    ([UUM-143645](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/selecting-delete-group-and-contents-in-sub-graph-throws-invalidoperationexception-when-the-output-node-is-included-in-the-group))

- Shadergraph: Improved responsiveness of node inspector for reflected function nodes.
    ([UUM-143249](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/graph-inspector-is-not-repainted-and-shader-include-errors-are-not-immediately-shown-to-users-when-editor-recompiles-and-shader-include-import-state-updates))

- Shadergraph: Limit the max number of cases on the switch node.
    ([UUM-143419](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-default-case-slash-breaks-the-switch-node))

- Shadergraph: Node selection state should restore properly on undo/redo for the graph inspector.
    ([UUM-142159](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/usability-selected-node-gets-unselected-when-undoing-changes-in-the-graph-inspector-node-settings-tab))

- Shadergraph: Removed the 'Clip' option, which was erroneously introduced, from the Screen Position node's dropdown.
    (UUM-151248)

- Shaders: \(Shaders\) The editor no longer hangs at "waiting for shader compilers" when asynchronous shader variant compilation is cancelled during domain reload or editor shutdown.

- Shaders: Enabled DXC shader compilation for Windows player builds when using the Linux or macOS Editor.

- Shaders: Fixed "Local keyword space index cache out of sync." errors and possible crashes when the Editor rendered asset previews after a shader reload.
    ([UUM-143941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143941))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash due to an out-of-range value being supplied to Shader global property APIs.
    ([UUM-150856](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150856))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when calling RenderingCommandBuffer.SetKeyword with null compute or ray tracing shader.
    ([UUM-158327](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158327))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when executing a RenderingCommandBuffer with a SetKeyword on a null or destroyed material.
    ([UUM-157105](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157105))

- Shaders: Fixed a player freeze when switching scenes repeatedly with a preloaded ShaderVariantCollection.
    ([UUM-156125](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156125))

- Shaders: Fixed a possible Editor crash when shader messages were read while a shader variant compiled in the background.
    (UUM-154510)

- Shaders: Fixed a random crash that could occur during shader variant uploads in Player builds.
    (UUM-150584)

- Shaders: Fixed a rare crash when doing a build.
    ([UUM-148715](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148715))

- Shaders: Fixed an issue where keywords on Materials and their variants were not updated until rendered in Scene view, Game view, or Inspector after a shader reimport.
    ([UUM-102156](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-102156))

- Shaders: Fixed crash in Editor when an uncompiled or broken shader is being rendered on a Canvas.
    ([UUM-153265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153265))

- Shaders: Fixed default-created LocalKeyword and LocalKeywordSpace field access crashing the Editor.
    ([UUM-156424](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156424))

- Shaders: Fixed editor crash by making UniqueIDGenerator thread-safe.
    (UUM-146812)

- Shaders: Fixed incorrect prompting of apply shader build settings window on removal of empty keywords override settings.
    ([UUM-148993](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148993))

- Shaders: Fixed material variants that kept an old property value after the base material changed, when an intermediate variant locked that property.
    ([UUM-158581](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158581))

- Shaders: Fixed Material.GetPropertyNames crashing when the provided value is outside of the valid enumeration range.
    ([UUM-157945](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157945))

- Shaders: Fixed preprocessor incorrectly skipping include files with multiple independent ifndef/endif guard blocks.
    (UUM-145788)

- Shaders: Fixed the shader keywords to work properly in cases where one pass declares keywords as multi_compile/shader_feature and another pass declares the same keywords as dynamic_branch.
    (UUM-139965)

- Shaders: Material.mainTexture will return null if the main texture is not present instead of logging an error in the console.
    ([UUM-103940](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-103940))

- Shaders: Shader preprocessor now handles short-circuiting evaluation.
    (UUM-135082)

- Shaders: Strip early subshaders if the `RenderPipeline` tag does not match any present renderpipeline assets for the build target.
    ([UUM-141340](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141340))

- Source Code Build: Fixed Editor source builds failing on machines that use the Turkish region format.
    (UUM-154501)

- SRP Core: Added a dedicated alpha upscale pass to preserve the alpha channel of the camera output.
    ([UUM-135242](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135242))

- SRP Core: Added a Hide/Show Overlays button to the Rendering Debugger, which temporarily hides all active debug overlays without losing the current selection.
    ([UUM-150066](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150066))

- SRP Core: BatchPackedCullingViewID::GetEntityID generates the EntityID using the ulong handle directly instead of an int mask.
    ([UUM-146214](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gpu-occlusion-culling-fails-to-cull-obstructed-instances-when-gpu-resident-drawer-is-enabled))

- SRP Core: Changed how support is checked for RT features in UnifiedRayTracing. The checks are now done using feature flags.
    (UUM-149856)

- SRP Core: Fixed a shader error when copying MSAA textures on OpenGL ES 3.1 devices that lack per-sample shading support; these devices now fall back to a regular blit.
    (UUM-147215)

- SRP Core: Fixed an assertion error and memory leak warning that were thrown when hiding a branch group on a Tree in a project that uses the GPU Resident Drawer.
    ([UUM-142959](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142959))

- SRP Core: Fixed an issue where the Convert Assets button in the Render Pipeline Converter window stayed disabled when you selected an unconverted item after a partial conversion.
    ([UUM-155720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155720))

- SRP Core: Fixed an issue where `Camera.RenderToCubemap` didn't capture Canvas geometry for the first face of a cubemap.
    ([UUM-140991](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140991))

- SRP Core: Fixed baking Adaptive Probe Volumes silently disabling Sky Occlusion in the Baking Set when no Lighting Settings Asset was assigned to the scene. With the deprecated Progressive CPU lightmapper, the Lighting window now shows a warning about double counted sky lighting instead of disabling the Sky Occlusion settings.
    ([UUM-148221](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148221))

- SRP Core: Fixed Blitter blits that use a custom material sampling at a mip level left over from an earlier blit, which showed an outdated image on some Android devices using OpenGL ES.
    (UUM-154958)

- SRP Core: Fixed cropped post-processing output when the targetTexture is swapped to a different size.
    ([UUM-145833](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145833))

- SRP Core: Fixed exceptions thrown in the Inspector when adding a Light Anchor component while no Game View is open.
    ([UUM-153999](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153999))

- SRP Core: Fixed low-contrast "ALL"/"NONE" override toggle text in the Volume component Inspector \(for example on Tonemapping, Bloom, or Motion Blur overrides\) that was nearly unreadable in the Light editor theme.
    ([UUM-149966](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149966))

- SRP Core: Fixed minor visual issue with Render Graph Viewer's Auto Update button.
    ([UUM-150051](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150051))

- SRP Core: Fixed Render Graph execution errors when a raster render pass binds 8 colour attachments and a depth attachment.
    ([UUM-149931](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149931))

- SRP Core: Fixed render passes not being merged on older iOS devices \(Apple GPU family 3 and below\) once a native pass was considered to be over the on-tile pixel storage limit, which serialized the whole frame into one native pass per render pass.
    ([UUM-148865](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148865))

- SRP Core: Fixed rendering corruption when using async compute with Render Graph, where a texture could be used from both the graphics and compute queues simultaneously.
    ([UUM-141896](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141896))

- SRP Core: Fixed the culled pass block being unreadable in the Render Graph Viewer in dark mode.
    ([UUM-146993](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146993))

- SRP Core: Fixed the first bake in Single Scene mode discarding Adaptive Probe Volume settings \(such as Sky Occlusion\) changed in the Lighting window before the Baking Set asset was created.
    (UUM-133186)

- SRP Core: Fixed the GPU Resident Drawer help box in the Rendering Debugger displaying C\# type names, such as "GPUResidentDrawer" and "RenderPipelineAsset", instead of readable names.
    ([UUM-138009](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138009))

- SRP Core: Fixed the Render Graph closing a native render pass after 8 raster render passes even when they used fewer than 8 native subpasses, which made the next pass, such as the URP UI overlay, load its attachments again from memory.
    ([UUM-158401](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158401))

- SRP Core: Fixed the Scan and Convert Assets buttons in the Render Pipeline Converter window being hidden when the window was narrower than its content.
    ([UUM-151491](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151491))

- SRP Core: Gate RenderGraph UV origin validation behind the `enableValidityChecks` flag to allow debug tools to bypass the check when intentionally creating conflicting UV origins.

- SRP Core: Removed a rare managed allocation during HDR output rendering that could intermittently occur when .NET reclaimed pooled array buffers between frames.
    (UUM-145447)

- SRP Core: Removed check to only consider emissives for GI if the _EMISSION keyword was added.

- SRP Core: Render Pipeline Converter - Keep state on assembly reloads.
    ([UUM-149926](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149926))

- SRP Core: Render Pipeline Converter - Scan button is always enabled as user can re-scan the project.
    ([UUM-151043](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151043))

- SRP Core: SRP Core: Fixed render area bug for FinalBlit pass when using dynamic resolution with renderScale 1.0.
    ([UUM-138644](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dynamic-resolution-no-longer-works-on-urp))

- SRP Core: \[Render Pipeline Converter\] Improve list views by adding categorization and sorting.
    ([UUM-146628](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146628))

- Terrain: Avoid buffer overflow when generating the buffers for the detail meshes.
    ([UUM-139959](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-memoryprofiler-getallocationroot-when-entering-play-mode-on-a-specific-project))

- Terrain: Fixed a crash in shadow culling when a render pass culled the same Camera a second time while a Terrain with `Draw Instanced` disabled was visible.
    ([UUM-151731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151731))

- Terrain: Fixed a crash when a script updates tree instances on a terrain whose tree prototypes cannot be instanced.
    ([UUM-150692](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150692))

- Terrain: Fixed a crash when loading a Terrain whose heightmap resolution exceeds the maximum render texture size of the device, for example a 4097 heightmap on a device limited to 4096.
    ([UUM-150551](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150551))

- Terrain: Fixed an "Overflow in memory allocator" fatal error when rendering a terrain with details from a camera extremely far away from it, such as a Scene view restored at a very distant position.
    ([UUM-155070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155070))

- Terrain: Fixed case of Terrain Layer property changes not being added to the Undo history when using the Universal Render Pipeline or the High Definition Render Pipeline.
    ([UUM-134483](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134483))

- Terrain: Fixed shader compilation errors that were thrown when a TerrainLit Shader Graph had no Block nodes in its Vertex Context.
    ([UUM-143598](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143598))

- Terrain: Fixed terrain painting being lost without any message when a new TerrainData was saved with `AssetDatabase.CreateAsset` after its terrain layers were assigned. A warning now explains the loss and how to avoid it.
    (UUM-155877)

- Terrain: Fixed the "Channel Remapping" foldout in the Terrain Layer Inspector not expanding when its title is clicked.
    ([UUM-148236](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148236))

- Terrain: Fixed the Edit Tree, Edit Detail Mesh and Edit Grass Texture windows applying changes to a different prototype when another prototype was removed while the window was open.
    ([UUM-155483](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155483))

- Terrain: Fixed the Editor hanging when painting terrain details in Coverage mode with a very small Min Width and Max Width, or with a detail mesh that is very thin along one axis.
    ([UUM-134662](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134662))

- Terrain: Fixed the Paint Texture tool keeping a replaced Terrain Layer selected, which showed the old layer's settings in the Inspector and added it back to the Terrain Layers list on the next paint stroke.
    ([UUM-134352](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134352))

- Terrain: Fixed the texture import warnings in the Terrain Layer Inspector being drawn over by the Color Tint, Opacity as Density and Normal Scale controls when using the Universal Render Pipeline or the High Definition Render Pipeline.
    ([UUM-133166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133166))

- Terrain: Fixed trees placed above or below the Terrain surface moving onto it when `SetHeights`, `SyncHeightmap`, a height brush, painting holes or changing the Terrain size modified another part of the Terrain.
    ([UUM-137127](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137127))

- Terrain: Fixed `IndexOutOfRangeException` being thrown when painting details on the edge or corner of a Terrain.
    ([UUM-149028](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149028))

- Test Framework: Async OneTimeSetup/OneTimeTearDown are now supported, fixing an issue where tests using it would hang or crash the editor.
    (UUM-128590)

- Test Framework: Fixed an issue where assembly-level `ITestAction` attributes with `ActionTargets.Test` were not invoked for individual test methods, and `ITest.Fixture` was null when accessed from `ITestAction` callbacks.
    ([UUM-137121](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137121))

- Test Framework: Fixed Is.Not.AllocatingGCMemory\(\) intermittently reporting GC allocations made by other threads, such as the finalizer thread after GC.Collect\(\), for a delegate that does not allocate.
    (UUM-142851)

- Test Framework: Profiler marker recorders used by `Measure.Method` now start when the measured iterations begin instead of when `ProfilerMarkers()` is called, so warmup and probing iterations no longer contribute to the reported marker values or add sample collection overhead to those phases.
    (UUM-147204)

- Test Framework: `Measure.Method().GC()` again forces a full garbage collection immediately before each timed region, restoring the behavior from before the switch to the `ProfilerRecorder` API. Without it, a collection triggered by the measured code's own allocations lands inside the timed region and shows up as periodic spikes across the samples. As before: this only happens when `GC()` is specified; warmup and probing iterations are not affected; and because `SetUp`/`CleanUp` cause each of the `IterationsPerMeasurement` inner executions to be timed separately, that combination gets one collection per inner execution rather than one per sample, which makes it considerably slower. Reported times for allocating methods measured with `GC()` will change, generally becoming lower and more stable, so baselines recorded since 3.2.1 are not comparable.
    (UUM-147204)

- Tests: Fixed an instability in the terrain test on Macs
    (UUM-153508)

- Tests: Re-enabled DispatchWithUniformUpdate test.
    (UGK-2462)

- Tests: Stablize flaky inspector header test.
    (UUM-142700)

- Text: Added font subsetting via importer recipes and hidden Font sub-assets.

- Text: Added glyph-to-character mapping to PostProcessTextVertices.
    (UUM-147627)

- Text: Added new SpaceWarp compatible shaders for TextMeshPro XR users.

- Text: Added support for the &lt;rotate&gt; rich text tag in the Advanced Text Generator.
    ([UUM-151216](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151216))

- Text: Defer OS Font Fallback loading.

- Text: Fixed "Failed to perform selection on text" in Console.
    ([UUM-149393](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149393))

- Text: Fixed a LegacyRuntime memory leak.
    (UUM-145108)

- Text: Fixed PostProcessTextVertices glyph enumeration out-of-range exception with rich text.

    ([UUM-146118](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146118))

    ([UUM-148143](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148143))

    ([UUM-135766](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135766))

- UI Toolkit: Hid internal UIToolkit Authoring controls from UI Library.

- UI Toolkit: Improved VisualElement.Add exception message when the element has no content container.
    ([UUM-147805](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147805))

- UI Toolkit: Made the Create &gt; UI Toolkit asset menu item names and default file names consistent.
    (UUM-133810)

- UI Toolkit: Parent template elements are no longer read-only after exiting context mode from a child template.
    (UUM-145920)

- UI Toolkit: Prevent some rare crash in UI Toolkit when repainting windows during a domain reload.
    (UUM-145384)

- UI Toolkit: Removed "No" option from some alert dialogs where it didn't apply.

- UI Toolkit: Removed the "backdrop-filter" label from the UI Builder Visual Element Inspector.
    ([UUM-145495](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/backdrop-filter-text-is-displayed-above-the-backdrop-filter-list-container-for-no-reason))

- UI Toolkit: Renaming the document row in the Hierarchy now renames the corresponding .uxml asset.

- UI Toolkit: Selecting "Custom Function" in the Backdrop Filter section of the Inspector no longer logs "Custom filters are not supported for backdrop-filter" warnings; the custom filter is now applied.
    ([UUM-148347](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148347))

- UI Toolkit: Selection highlight no longer remains in the UI Viewport after deleting the hovered element or style rule from the Hierarchy.
    ([UUM-144573](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144573))

- UI Toolkit: Surface the backdrop-filter Screen Space-only limitation with a documentation icon in the Backdrop Filter inspector sections and on the PanelSettings Render Mode field.
    (UUM-147138)

- UI Toolkit: The "Save As" field in the "Save USS File" dialog is now pre-filled with a default name \("New USS"\) when creating a new StyleSheet from the UI Builder or the in-scene UI Toolkit editing stage's Style Sheets window.
    ([UUM-150992](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150992))

- UI Toolkit: Tooltips in Inspector when in UIStaging don't relay leaf element tooltips.
    (UUM-145049)

- UI Toolkit: UI Builder: Fixed the data source path completer popup in the binding window sometimes staying open after the window was moved.
    (UUM-132416)

- UI Toolkit: Updated the inspector field in stage to match the existing renaming behavior.
    ([UUM-153274](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153274))

- UI Toolkit: VisualElement Inspector Open in Context button corrupts UI Viewport window.
    (UUM-145658)

- Undo System: Undo selections and save importer changes without errors.
    ([UUM-148141](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148141))

- Unity Physics: Added a check for the MostRecentFixedTime entity in the physics GraphicsIntegration code to remove the entity if its DynamicBuffer is zero. The CameraSmoothTrack system now has an dependency on MostRecentFixedTime.
    ([UUM-145828](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145828))

- Unity Physics: Added a \[NoAutoStaticsCleanup\] attribute to some of the scripts in the 'Custom Physics Authoring' sample to avoid UAL0010 and UAL0013 warnings upon import. The behaviour of the scripts remains unchanged.

- Unity Physics: Broadphase.Tree.Clone\(\) now allocates node storage that is sized to its capacity rather than the source tree's Length. This fixes an OutOfRange error that may be observed when a full multi-threaded broadphase rebuild is performed on a densely-packed, cloned world.
    (UUM-157069)

- Unity Physics: Fixed a race condition so that DebugDraw jobs from the previous frame are completed before the stream are cleared for re-use.
    (UUM-152608)

- Unity Physics: Fixed an issue where casting a collider away form another one could return the wrong hit.
    (UUM-138425)

- Unity Physics: Fixed an issue where the BVH static tree wouldn't be updated correctly when swapping from multi-threaded to single threaded setups.
    (UUM-133011)

- Unity Physics: Regenerated some sample meta files to fix serialized versioning warnings when the 'Advanced Game Physics Sample - Chain and Gears' sample was imported.
    ([UUM-149515](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149515))

- Unity Physics: Vertex counting to select the correct rendering index format for a convex mesh that has more than 65535 vertices when using a combined mesh.
    (UUM-158827)

- Unity Physics: `Collider.ToMesh()` leaked an intermediate mesh for every convex, mesh and terrain collider it converted. These meshes are now destroyed once they are combined into the returned mesh.
    (UUM-158827)

- Universal Windows Platform: Fixed scroll actions re-triggering on mouse movement on UWP.
    ([UUM-144660](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/uwp-mouse-movement-re-triggers-stale-slash-scroll-value-after-the-scroll-wheel-is-used-once))

- URP: Converting BiRP Particles materials to URP now correctly preserves emission state. Previously, the emission checkbox would appear enabled in the inspector even when emission was disabled in the source material.
    ([UUM-128093](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rpconverter-converting-particles-material-enables-the-emission-checkbox))

- URP: Ensure command line URP converter scanning saves its report to a persistent location.
    (UUM-155565)

- URP: Fix to skip the SSAO pass when the active renderer does not provide the camera depth/normals textures.
    ([UUM-150219](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150219))

- URP: Fixed "Default" being added to some Enum Material properties.
    ([UUM-134256](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134256))

- URP: Fixed a bug where lights render with incorrect shape when using WebGL with Chromium running with DX11 backend.
    ([UUM-142771](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142771))

- URP: Fixed a bug where reloading a scene that contains embedded meshes causes Surface Cache GI to stop working.
    (UUM-152963)

- URP: Fixed a bug where reloading a scene would cause certain `Blitter` functions to stop working.
    (UUM-153092)

- URP: Fixed a bug where seams appear on box projected reflection probes on platforms with half precision \(mobile\).
    ([UUM-141981](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141981))

- URP: Fixed a bug where URP attempts to use a stripped variant when using deferred rendering with additional light shadows disabled, but shadowmasks enabled.
    ([UUM-153600](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153600))

- URP: Fixed a crash on some iOS devices when using the Forward+ rendering path with Render Scale not set to 1.
    ([UUM-151281](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151281))

- URP: Fixed a Render Graph error when Tile-Only Mode was enabled on displays with a non-sRGB backbuffer, such as OpenGL ES in Linear color space. Tile-Only Mode now falls back to regular rendering on those displays.
    (UUM-147232)

- URP: Fixed a spelling mistake where the Screen Space Lens Flare "Chromatic Aberration" setting was labelled "Chromatic Abberation".
    ([UUM-146130](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146130))

- URP: Fixed Accurate G-buffer Normals encode/decode mismatch in Decals.
    ([UUM-146733](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146733))

- URP: Fixed an issue where a Render Pipeline Converter scan did not list assets that were created or modified just before the scan started.
    (UUM-154992)

- URP: Fixed an issue where changing Shadow Layers with multiple Lights selected only updated the first Light, and undo did not revert it. Lights in scenes saved before this fix might keep outdated shadow layers if they were edited this way; set their Shadow Layers again to update them.
    ([UUM-157356](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157356))

- URP: Fixed an issue where the "Reflect Sky" setting in URP Screen Space Reflections would cause artifacts when the reflected point lies behind a piece of geometry.
    (UUM-146458)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter's Animation Clip converter sometimes did not report animation clips used by an Animator Controller, so repeated scans of an unchanged project returned different results.
    (UUM-154762)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter's Post-Processing Stack v2 converter skipped GameObjects in scenes that were not open.
    ([UUM-151409](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151409))

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Renderer List and Culling samples in the URP RenderGraph Samples logged errors every frame when used with a 2D Renderer that has Depth/Stencil Buffer disabled.
    ([UUM-157948](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157948))

- URP: Fixed blocky gaps at the edges of spot lights in Forward+ and Deferred+ when the light is attached to the camera.
    ([UUM-154016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154016))

- URP: Fixed Bloom intensity getting overridden by Screen Space Lens Flare intensity.
    (UUM-158619)

- URP: Fixed case of ShaderGraph terrain writing zero depth to the GBuffer.
    ([UUM-140545](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140545))

- URP: Fixed case of the deprecated `LightingPhysicallyBased` overload ignoring the surface normal and using the specular highlights toggle as the view direction.
    (UUM-149002)

- URP: Fixed case of the Render Pipeline Converter's 2D Material Reference and Shader Converters naming the renderer inconsistently \(as "Renderer 2D"\) in their disabled tooltip, instead of matching the Target Pipeline dropdown's "2D Renderer".
    ([UUM-151380](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151380))

- URP: Fixed Custom Interpolators not flowing from the vertex to fragment stage in Decal Shader Graphs.
    ([UUM-117270](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-117270))

- URP: Fixed Data-driven Lens Flare occlusion under an orthographic camera.
    (UUM-142763)

- URP: Fixed Editor flickering and blank windows when a camera's Output Texture is a Render Texture with Shadow Sampling Mode set to Compare Depths.
    ([UUM-155764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155764))

- URP: Fixed forward-only materials, such as Complex Lit, not receiving additional light shadows when the Rendering Path is set to Deferred and Transparent Receive Shadows is disabled.
    ([UUM-155328](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155328))

- URP: Fixed Gizmos rendering outside of the camera's viewport in the Game view when using URP.
    ([UUM-131882](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131882))

- URP: Fixed invalid colors in Lift, Gamma, Gain post-processing by clamping negative colors in LUTBuilder shaders.
    ([UUM-148848](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148848))

- URP: Fixed lights missing from parts of the screen with the Forward+ or Deferred+ rendering path for some spot light orientations and for spot lights close to the camera's near plane.

- URP: Fixed long player build times in 2D projects caused by unneeded Rendering Layer Mask shader variants in Sprite Lit, Sprite Custom Lit, and VFX Graph shaders.
    (UUM-158073)

- URP: Fixed missing shadows on certain shadow casting point and spot lights, in scenes with a large number of lights using the Deferred renderer when Transparent Receive Shadows was enabled.
    (UUM-134596)

- URP: Fixed missing SpeedTree7 bark on PowerVR Vulkan with a never-firing clip.
    (UUM-155486)

- URP: Fixed missing tile map grid lines. Ensure preview camera render target is correct after rendering with an SRP.
    ([UUM-122618](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-122618))

- URP: Fixed MSAA error in the Rendering Debugger Motion Vector view.
    ([UUM-142923](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142923))

- URP: Fixed multi-display support in standalone URP 2D/3D applications.
    (UUM-134688)

- URP: Fixed normal maps getting ignored during prepass when Accurate G-buffer Normals were enabled.
    ([UUM-146688](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146688))

- URP: Fixed point and spot lights disappearing near screen edges and the camera near plane in Forward+ and Deferred+.
    ([UUM-146356](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146356))

- URP: Fixed Project Auditor URP settings issues for Quality Levels naming the Quality Level instead of the assigned URP asset.
    (UUM-155704)

- URP: Fixed shader resolve for XR is not enabled properly in on-tile postprocessing.
    (UUM-143511)

- URP: Fixed spot lights behind the camera losing the top of their lit area when using the Forward+ or Deferred+ rendering path.
    ([UUM-159132](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159132))

- URP: Fixed Surface Cache Global Illumination producing incorrect or missing bounce lighting in player builds that use vertex compression.
    (UUM-155591)

- URP: Fixed the 2D Renderer creating a depth and stencil buffer in players when Disable Depth and Stencil Buffers is enabled in Player Settings.
    ([UUM-150232](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150232))

- URP: Fixed the Pixel Perfect Camera converter in the Render Pipeline Converter staying disabled and reporting the 2D Pixel Perfect package as not installed when it was installed.
    (UUM-154777)

- URP: Fixed the Render Pipeline Converter defaulting its Target Pipeline to Universal Render Pipeline \(2D Renderer\) instead of Universal Render Pipeline \(Universal Renderer\).
    ([UUM-151492](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151492))

- URP: Improved GPU performance for URP projects on tile-based platforms by avoiding unnecessary color loads when UberPostProcessing is the final pass.
    (UUM-140408)

- URP: Increment UpdateCount of RenderTextures in URP.
    ([UUM-139389](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139389))

- URP: Reduced back buffer memory and bandwidth on platforms with a multisampled back buffer including Android, iOS, and Metal. The back buffer remains single-sampled in frames where no camera renders MSAA directly to it, for example a camera that resolves MSAA from an intermediate texture. MSAA results are unaffected.
    (UUM-134600)

- URP: Rendering Debugger - GPU Resident Drawer was resetting the debug data when entering and exiting playmode.
    ([UUM-143210](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/renderingdebugger-rendering-debugger-window-resets-when-entering-playmode))

- URP: ScriptableRendererData.TryGetRendererFeature was throwing NullReferenceException.
    ([UUM-150772](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150772))

- URP: Skip renderer culling for cameras with an empty culling mask to reduce overhead from cameras rendering no geometry.

- URP: URP Render Graph Memoryless backbuffer support on iOS Metal.
    (UUM-99364)

- URP: URP Renderer Asset - Undo/redo operations on `FullScreenPassRendererFeature_OldGUID` caused to clear the Undo/Redo stack.
    ([UUM-143675](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143675))

- URP: URP Renderer Asset - Undo/redo operations on `FullScreenPassRendererFeature_OldGUID` caused `ObjectDisposedException` errors and prevented undoing renderer feature additions.
    ([UUM-147579](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147579))

- URP: When using Tile-Only mode in the renderer, setting Render Scale in the asset's Quality settings would display a warning only for values &lt;0.95 or &gt;1.05, but this has been fixed to display for all non-default values when Render Scale is not compatible with Tile-Only mode.
    ([UUM-146401](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146401))

- URP: \[MaterialConverter\] Particles Unlit Rendering Mode Subtractive gives empty Surface Type dropdown after the conversion.
    ([UUM-133453](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133453))

- Version Control: Fixed an issue where unchanged files were automatically added to the change list when fetching files in Perforce after asset importing.
    ([UUM-49713](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-49713))

- Version Control: Fixed race condition provoking a Version Control error "UpdateSettings: Unknown version control plugin: XYZ" during the editor startup.
    ([UUM-149884](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149884))

- VFX Graph: Added issue where NaNs could perpetually keep a graph in a dirty state.
    ([UUM-139398](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139398))

    ([UUM-151393](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151393))

    ([UUM-151382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151382))

    ([UUM-151379](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151379))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a crash that occurred in VFXManager::SortBufferCommand on devices with low \(16KB\) thread group shared memory.
    ([UUM-136592](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136592))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a GPU crash or corrupted rendering with sorted Visual Effect Graph outputs when some instances of an effect were outside a camera's view, especially with several cameras.

- VFX Graph: Fixed an exception that occurred when enabling the template toggle.
    ([UUM-149239](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149239))

- VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where nested OnPlay, OnStop, and similar events resulted in stack overflows.
    ([UUM-121002](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121002))

- VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where output property nodes would cause errors when converted to operator subgraphs.
    ([UUM-108536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-108536))

- VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where template attributes may fail silently on insertion.
    ([UUM-121263](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121263))

- VFX Graph: Fixed blackboard "Attributes" subsections being expanded and collapsed when navigating with arrow keys.
    ([UUM-144932](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144932))

- VFX Graph: Fixed blackboard custom attribute delete doing nothing on an in-use attribute when re-triggered after cancelling the deletion confirmation prompt.
    ([UUM-145053](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145053))

- VFX Graph: Fixed hover highlight of items in toolbar dropdown menus being hard to see in the Light Editor theme.
    ([UUM-139764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139764))

- VFX Graph: Fixed missing ellipses in Create Visual Effect Graph menu entry.
    ([UUM-145005](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-ellipses-are-used-for-the-visual-effect-graph-create-context-menu-option))

- VFX Graph: Fixed renaming a category will move its position to the end of the list when saving
    ([UUM-151383](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151383))

- VFX Graph: Fixed templates not appearing immediately when pressing the "New..." button in the Shader Graph Output context after entering Play Mode.
    ([UUM-143681](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143681))

- VFX Graph: Fixed the play rate presets in the VFX Control panel showing a "Ctrl+" prefix.
    ([UUM-138660](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138660))

- VFX Graph: Fixed VFX Graph windows prompting to save a second time after a Save All operation closed them.
    (UUM-131449)

    ([UUM-147335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147335))

- VFX Graph: Renaming a Category to a reserved name now logs an error message instead of failing silently
    ([UUM-151381](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151381))

- VFX Graph: Renaming a custom attribute now propagates validation state to custom HLSL blocks correctly.

    ([UUM-152713](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152713))

    ([UUM-152711](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152711))

    ([UUM-135548](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135548))

- Video: Fixed a VideoPlayer freeze for looping clips that could occur upon entering Playmode, where playback would stop and not recover.

    ([UUM-143660](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/audiosampleprovider-dot-sampleframesavailable-callback-never-fires-when-using-macos))

    (UUM-145959)

- Video: Fixed WebM Vorbis audio track reporting 0 Hz and 0 channels.
    (UUM-144045)

- Video: Make VideoPlayer_TimeReference_Works pass by fixing the way we detect when to run loopPointReached.
    (UUM-148456)

- Video: \[Android\] A crash caused by Vulkan video decoding workaround being applied incorrectly.
    ([UUM-145932](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145932))

- VisionOS: Fix exporting visionOS Xcode project fails after disabling Symlink Sources
    (UUM-154567)

- Web: Added warnings when scripted change to maximum memory size results in memory size clamping or switch to WebAssembly 2023 target.
    ([UUM-151511](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151511))

- Web: Blocked input events during synchronous scene loading so they aren't processed in the new scene.

    ([UUM-151065](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151065))

- Web: Fixed issue where editor crashes on exit when a manual proxy without credentials is configured on Windows.

- Web: Fixed Mouse and Touchscreen being registered on Input System startup when not present.
    (UUM-150405)

- Web: Fixed NameFilesAsHashes functionality.
    (UUM-148663)

- Web: Fixed NameFilesAsHashes when building in batchmode.
    ([UUM-149003](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149003))

- Web: Fixed scene loading race conditions when using progressive asset loading.

- Web: Fixed stack overflow in Adb.Exec/DumpLogInternal recursion.
    (UUM-148540)

- Web: Fixed streaming of response body in UnityWebRequest when server does not set "Content-Length" header.
    ([UUM-146537](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146537))

- Web: Fixed UnityHttpMessageHandler leaking its Abort\(\) cancellation registration.
    ([UUM-157566](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157566))

- Web: Fixed WASM_SIZE, wasmFileSize that is used in memory profiler.
    (UUM-149075)

- Web: Fixed Web platform build when output asset sizes are larger than 2 GB, and the build error message if output asset sizes are larger than 4 GB \(unsupported\).
    ([UUM-112909](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-112909))

- WebGL: Added JSPI support to enable WebGPU sync readback functions.

- WebGL: Fixed a crash and "multisampled flag doesn't match" validation errors on the WebGPU backend when render targets were recreated, for example while resizing the canvas with matchWebGLToCanvasSize enabled.
    ([UUM-149827](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149827))

- WebGL: Fixed an issue where ASTC-compressed textures with multiple layers and mipmaps were having their texture data uploaded incorrectly.
    (UUM-145086)

- WebGL: Fixed error if shader expects a cube texture but a texture wasn't explicitly bound.
    ([UUM-150746](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150746))

- WebGL: Fixed issue in WebGPU Device Filter editor control layout.
    ([UUM-146418](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146418))

- WebGL: Improved performance by using the device native format for the intermediate color texture.
    ([UUM-150744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150744))

- WebGL: WebGPU Fix compressed texture mipmaps.
    ([UUM-155238](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155238))

- WebGL: WebGPU fix for incorrectly mapped ASTC sRGB formats.
    (UUM-154502)

- WebGL: WebGPU: Fix error on some mobile devices due to the lack of the float32-blendable WebGPU extension.
    ([UUM-145735](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgpu-urp-errors-when-float32-blendable-isnt-available))

- WebGL: WebGPU: improve memory usage of uniform buffers and dynamic vertex buffers
    (UUM-152972)

- WebGL: \[WebGPU\] Fix crash that can occur when destroying and creating shaders.
    ([UUM-145390](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/type-error-is-thrown-when-a-new-computeshader-is-instantiated-after-destroying-previous-computeshader))

- WebGL: \[WebGPU\] Fixed case of render_attachment usage flags being set on textures when not needed, potentially affecting performance of those textures.
    (UUM-150892)

- Windows: Added CoreCLR architecture-dependent managed assembly support to Windows player build pipeline, including Visual Studio solution export.

- Windows: Fixed a top-level menu returning to the wrong position in the menu bar after being removed and restored.

- Windows: Fixed an issue where truncated reads over 32MiB through the AsyncReadManager would fail with Direct Storage enabled.
    (UUM-149542)

- Windows: Fixed an issue where Windows pen input did not generate mouse pointer events \(`Input.GetMouseButtonDown`\) when using the Input Manager in standalone Player builds.
    ([UUM-140737](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/windows-pen-input-does-not-generate-mouse-pointer-events-when-using-input-manager))

- Windows: Fixed case of plugin errors being logged when logging is disabled on Windows.
    ([UUM-149022](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149022))

- Windows: Fixed display activation failing after introducing the GameWindow API.

- Windows: Fixed GameWindow reporting client rendering area position instead of window frame position

- Windows: Fixed lag when dragging an object over docked window tabs in the Windows Editor.
    ([UUM-156431](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156431))

- Windows: Fixed On Demand Rendering related issues when hiding and resizing embedded window.
    ([UUM-102668](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-102668))

- Windows: Fixed the splash screen not fading out smoothly on start.
    ([UUM-140141](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/win-player-splashscreen-does-not-fade-into-the-first-scene-unlike-shown-in-the-preview))

- XR: Fixed an issue where URP scenes rendered black on Meta Quest 2 with Vulkan and MSAA, for example scenes with decals and no transparent objects.
    (UUM-132022)

- XR: Fixed blocky or shifted lighting artifacts that could appear on XR platforms especially when using dynamic resolution in Deferred or Forward+ rendering paths.
    (UUM-143709)

- XR: Fixed SubsystemWithProvider.Start not throwing an ArgumentNullException when called after the subsystem was destroyed, which restarted the destroyed provider.
    ([UUM-151411](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151411))

- XR: Fixed XR graphics becoming frozen when changing the MSAA level at runtime, while having depth textures submission enabled.

- XR: Now when using intermediate textures in URP \(such as with post-processing\), the XR eye texture will no longer force MSAA when enabled which previously incurred a performance cost with no additional benefit due to already resolving to 1x sampling before the final pass.
    (UUM-155648)

- XR: OnDemandRendering.effectiveRenderFrameRate returns the correct refresh rate on XR devices instead of a default value.

- XR: Quad Views: Fixed issue where lights were culled within view when forward+ was enabled, causing artifacts in the periphery.
    ([UUM-153433](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153433))

- XR: Removed AABB boundary expansion from SplitCullObjectsWithoutUmbra.

- XR: XR Core Utilities: Fixed the Project Validation window so that it never tries to display NaN amount of progress to the Editor's progress bar.

- XR: XR Plug-in Management: Fixed the merge behavior of ManifestRequirement.OverrideElements, as used by IAndroidManifestRequirementProvider:<br>
    - Repeatable elements identified by name, such as meta-data, uses-feature and activity, with a "name" key only overwrite elements with a matching "name" key. Elements that can only occur once, such as application, are still overwritten in place when a "name" key is given.<br>
    - Elements with conflicting true/false values for the "required" key resolve to "true".<br>
    -Elements with conflicting values for the "version" key resolve to the highest provided version number.




#### Preview of Final 6000.7.0b9 Package changes
